It groups by (sale_id, item_id, unit_cost) in SQL and looks a cost up once per DISTINCT item that lacks a snapshot. Required adding `refunded_net` to both the aggregate and the row query.
- **Who sees profit (1.94.0)**: `/sales` GET is `$auth` (the till calls it), so cost is shaped OUT of the payload below manager - `totals.profit/cost/margin_pct` come back **null**, not 0. The front end derives `showProfit` from whether the figure actually arrived rather than from the role name, and drops the tiles, the table column AND the CSV columns together - a header with no cells skews the table.
- **iOS focus zoom (1.93.3)**: on iOS, focusing a text field whose COMPUTED font-size is under 16px zooms the whole page - and it never zooms back out, so the till sat magnified with the scan button and half the grid off-screen. Every field was under the threshold: `.vgc-sm-pos__search` 15px, `.vgc-sm-input` 14px, `.vgc-sm-posdisc__v` 13px, `.vgc-sm-posprice input` 14px. Worse, pos.js calls `.focus()` PROGRAMMATICALLY in three places (the discount value after each re-render, and the manual-scan input twice), so it fired without the user even tapping a field. Fixed with a `@media (pointer: coarse)` block **appended at the END of app.css** raising every input/textarea/select to 16px - last in the file so it wins on source order against the equally-specific per-screen rules (`.vgc-sm-of .vgc-sm-input` etc. are 0,2,0, same as `.vgc-sm .vgc-sm-input`). **Do not add a smaller font-size after that block.** Deliberately NOT solved with `maximum-scale=1`: that disables pinch-zoom for everyone, and Safari ignores it outside standalone anyway. Desktop is untouched (coarse-pointer only) - verified at 1280px where fields stay at 14px. scratchpad/iosinputs.js walks every field on a 390px iPhone viewport and fails if any computes under 16px.
- **Till photo full-bleed + phone columns (1.93.2)**: `aspect-ratio` was NOT enough. The image box is a flex item, so its height stayed indefinite; the img's `height:100%` had nothing to resolve against, and `min-height:96px` became the real height - a fixed box with a short picture in it. Replaced with the bulletproof ratio box: `position:relative; width:100%; height:0; padding-top:75%; flex:none` (padding percentages resolve against WIDTH, so the height always tracks the column) plus `position:absolute; top:0; left:0; width:100%; height:100%; object-fit:cover` on the img, and a separately centred `svg` rule so the no-photo placeholder is not stretched. Also: `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(150px,1fr))` drops to ONE column below ~360px (two need 312px), giving a single product per row with a ~294px photo - so `@media (max-width:400px)` pins `repeat(2, minmax(0,1fr))`.
- **Verifying mobile layout (1.93.2)**: headless Chrome on Windows CLAMPS the window to ~500px and merely CROPS the screenshot, and `--dump-dom` ignores `--window-size` entirely - so any phone-width screenshot taken that way is a lie (it reported vw=500 while the PNG was 320px wide). Use puppeteer-core against the installed Chrome with `page.setViewport({isMobile:true})`, and **give the harness page the same `<meta name="viewport">` the app ships** or emulation falls back to a 980px layout viewport. scratchpad/phone.js measures cols/tile/photo/full-bleed across 320-430px.
- **Till tile proportions (1.93.1)**: `.vgc-sm-postile__img` was a fixed `height: 62px` while `__name` had no row limit — so a long product name grew the tile and the photo kept its 62px, shrinking as a proportion of it. The photo is what a seller actually scans for on a stall, so it is now `aspect-ratio: 4 / 3` (with `min-height: 96px` where aspect-ratio is unsupported), scaling with the column, and `__name` is clamped to two rows via `-webkit-line-clamp` plus `overflow-wrap: anywhere` (an unbroken string would otherwise overflow rather than wrap). The tile gained a `title` attribute so a clamped name is still readable, and the no-photo placeholder icon went 22px→34px so it does not float in the larger box. Verified by rendering the real template against the real app.css in headless Chrome at both desktop and 375px widths — layout is not testable in jsdom.
- **One installable PWA per sale location (1.93.0)**: virtual files `vgc-till-{id}.webmanifest` and `vgc-till-{id}-{192|512}.png`, matched in `serve_pwa_assets()` by prefixing `asset_path('')` (works at site root and in a subdirectory). **`id` is what separates the installs** — same origin, same `scope`, different identity; without a distinct `id` a phone treats them as one app. `scope` stays the APP root, not the till URL: a query string cannot narrow a scope, and anything tighter pushes the till's own navigations out of the installed window. `start_url` carries `vgc_sm_pos=1&vgc_sm_loc={id}`; colour comes from `till_colour()`, which mirrors `locKind()` in locations.tpl.js.
- **Till icons are generated (1.93.0)**: GD draws the location's initials on its kind colour, because three identical 'Stock' icons on one home screen make the whole feature useless. `till_initials()` carries its own **Cyrillic→Latin map**: `remove_accents()` does NOT transliterate Cyrillic and GD's built-in font cannot draw it, so without the map every Bulgarian location fell back to its id number. Digraphs keep only their leading Latin letter (Ж→Z, Ш→S) — collisions are cheaper than an unreadable icon. Falls back to the app icon where GD is absent. iOS ignores manifest icons for Add to Home Screen, so `touch_icon()` makes `<link rel="apple-touch-icon">` follow whichever manifest the page wears.
- **Pinning + the two things that would have broken it (1.93.0)**: the till is PINNED server-side — `render_pos()` resolves `vgc_sm_loc`, ships `boot.locId`/`boot.locked`, and pos.js hides the switcher AND skips binding its handler, so the front end cannot talk itself out of the lock. (1) **Login discarded the destination**: `handle_login()` ended in `wp_safe_redirect( self::url() )` unconditionally, so a seller opening their till app landed in the back office — the exact thing the feature exists to avoid. The login form's action now carries the flag and the integer location id, and the URL is REBUILT server-side rather than followed, so it cannot become an open redirect. (2) **The service worker sent every offline navigation to the app shell** — a till at a market with no signal showed the back office. Till pages are now cached as they are visited and an offline navigation matches the request first.
- **location_services was never created (1.92.3)**: the CREATE TABLE went into `ensure_pos_tables()`, which is guarded by `vgc_sm_pos_tables_repaired_v1` — an option ALREADY SET on every existing install, so it never ran. Every INSERT then failed and `add_service()` returned true regardless, so the UI reported a save that never happened. Fixes: the table is now also in `create_tables()` via dbDelta (fresh installs), the `vgc_sm_services_v1` guard calls `ensure_pos_tables()`, a second guard `vgc_sm_services_table_v2` covers installs that took 1.92.0-1.92.2 (their services_v1 flag is already set without the table), and `add_service()` checks the query result — `INSERT IGNORE` returns 0 for an existing row but FALSE when the statement fails. **Rule to preserve: a one-time repair guard is not a place to add NEW schema — every new table or column needs its own fresh option key, and a write whose failure the UI reports as success will hide it for weeks.**
- **Open till carried no location (1.92.3)**: `boot.posUrl` is a bare `?vgc_sm_pos=1` URL and `pos.js` did `state.locId = state.locations[0].id` unconditionally, so every Open till button opened the FIRST active location. `tillUrl(locId)` in locations.tpl.js appends `vgc_sm_loc=<id>` (joining with & since posUrl already has a query), and pos.js honours it only when it names a location that is actually in the active list — unknown id, closed location, rubbish or no parameter all fall back to the first, which is what a bare till URL and every older link mean. **Only the DETAIL page's button carries an id**: `listPage()` has no `loc` in scope, and putting `tillUrl(loc.id)` there throws at render — caught in the harness.
- **Push picker vs services (1.92.2)**: `push()` refuses a service with the right message, but nobody ever saw it - the CLIENT check `if (qty <= 0) toast('Enter a quantity')` fires before any request, and `pushStaged` renders the quantity input as `max="<workshop stock>"`, which is 0 for a service. So the field could never hold a usable number and the only reachable outcome was a message about the wrong thing. Fixes: the picker filters services out of pushable rows but still NAMES them under the results (`pushServiceHint`) pointing at the Services-offered-here card - hiding them would leave a searcher convinced the item does not exist; the picker row carries `data-service`; the staged row checks `is_service` BEFORE quantity (only reachable if the item became a service with the screen open); and any row with `avail <= 0` now renders 'nothing at the workshop' instead of an unusable field plus a Push button. **Rule to preserve: when a server-side guard exists, check the client-side ordering too - a generic client validation that runs first will mask the specific server message every time.**
- **Stranded stock + the directional rule (1.92.1)**: an item pushed to a location and only THEN marked a service left units that could not be sold correctly nor recovered. Two causes. (1) `inventory()` hard-coded `is_service = false` on every stock row, so the leftover `location_stock` row rendered as an ordinary product tile with a quantity - and if the service was also on the menu it appeared twice. Rows now carry the item's real flag plus **`row_type`: 'stock' | 'stranded' | 'service'**; the till filters out `stranded`, the location screen splits on `row_type` (stranded rows stay in the STOCK table, because that is where Pull back lives), and both inventory renderers tag them. (2) `pull()` refused services outright AND calls `record_movement()`, whose blanket service refusal would have written the location ledger row while silently failing the workshop movement - **losing the units**. **The rule is now directional: a service may never GAIN stock, but stock it already has must always be able to leave.** `record_movement` refuses only `qty > 0` and only when `type !== 'from_location'` (units coming home are a transfer of existing stock, not new stock); `create_movement` moved its check AFTER the switch so it tests the computed `$delta`. The item page mirrors it: Scrap + Set qty reappear for a service only while `stock_qty > 0`, with a line saying how much is stranded, and vanish once it is clear. Receive is never offered. Rule to preserve: a blanket refusal on a choke point is wrong when the choke point is also the only exit.
- **Services (1.92.0, DB 0.31.0)**: `items.is_service` - a product that is SOLD but never counted. Deliberately a flag, NOT a new `kind`: kind is raw/manufactured and drives recipes, production and costing (`kind === 'manufactured'` is tested everywhere), while is_service is about whether stock is COUNTED. `sanitize_item` forces `for_sale = 1` alongside it, so the two can never disagree. **The invariant: a service has no ledger.** Every stock write funnels through `record_movement()`, which now returns a WP_Error for one - the backstop; the legitimate callers skip BEFORE calling (orders set `$want_out = 0`, notes refuse the line outright) so the backstop only fires on a path nobody thought of. `create_movement`, location push/pull and the shop push all refuse one with their own message.
- **The per-location service menu (1.92.0)**: new table `vgc_sm_location_services (location_id, item_id)`. **Not a location_stock row**: that table is a cache `sync_stock_cache()` recomputes and `rebuild_stock_cache()` truncates, so a zero-qty service row would be erased by the next repair - and Reset lists location_stock as transaction data. The menu is catalogue data, so it is in `Reset::kept_tables()` and survives a test-data wipe. API: `Locations::services()` (shaped like inventory rows but `qty: null`, `is_service: true`), `offers_service()`, `add_service()`, `remove_service()`, `service_locations()`. `inventory()` returns stocked rows PLUS the menu, name-sorted - the till wants them together, the management screen splits them again on `is_service` (a quantity column means nothing for a service; the CSV filters them out too). Checkout swaps the on-hand check for an is-it-offered check; `deduct_for_sale`/`return_from_sale` are skipped. Routes: GET/POST `/locations/{id}/services`, DELETE `/locations/{id}/services/{item}`. `item_locations` returns `service_locations()` for a service, so the item page's card answers WHERE rather than how much.
- **Service UI rules (1.92.0)**: `onHand()` in pos.js returns **Infinity** for a service - not a fudge but the honest answer, and it makes every existing "is there enough" comparison pass without special-casing. The tile is never `disabled` and shows `Service` in place of a quantity (`.vgc-sm-postile__stock--svc`). The item page swaps the big number for "Always available", drops every stock action, hides the reorder level, and - the leak found in testing - must also drop the stock card's "At sale locations" TOTAL, which summed `l.qty` and rendered NaN. Shop pull maps the Bridge's `virtual` flag to is_service (absent flag = normal stocked product, so an old Bridge still works).
- **Produce list: own card, paged, recipes first (1.91.0)**: the results moved OUT of `#p-pick` into their own card `#p-listcard` (`data-devid="produce-list"`, holding `#p-count` / `#p-results` / `#p-pager`) - which means `pick()` must now hide it explicitly, because it used to disappear as collateral when `p-pick.innerHTML` was replaced. `PER_PAGE = 25` with the standard `.vgc-sm-pager` (Previous / `Page x / y - n products` / Next); paging is pure client-side slicing of `view`, no refetch. State is `view` (already ordered) + `note` + `page`; `show()` re-orders and ALWAYS resets to page 1 (staying on page 3 of a list that just shrank shows nothing), `render()` only slices. `ordered()` sorts `has_bom === false` last then by name - **only an explicit false demotes**, so a cached payload without the flag does not push everything into the no-recipe half. `--tall` was dropped here (pagination replaced the inner scroll) but is still used by the recipe Add picker.
- **Pickers list first, search narrows (1.90.0)**: the Produce picker and the recipe Add-component picker both rendered NOTHING until something was typed. Both now fetch their pool once (`limit=500`, the repository ceiling) and filter it in the browser - instant, no per-keystroke request, works offline once the screen is open. **The truncation guard matters**: when the fetched set reaches the ceiling it MAY be incomplete, so those installs fall back to server-side search rather than filtering a partial set (Produce: `truncated` flag; recipe: `addPool.length >= POOL_MAX`). The recipe pool is cached on the outer `viewRecipe` scope so it survives the re-render each added component triggers, and is only cached from an UNFILTERED response - typing before the first load lands would otherwise cache a search result. Produce lists products with no recipe too (they CAN be produced, consuming nothing) tagged 'no recipe' off `has_bom`. `.vgc-sm-results--tall` caps the list at min(52vh,460px) and scrolls it so the plan below stays reachable.
- **Bulk recipe lookup (1.90.0)**: `shape_item()` called `get_bom($id)` per row, so listing 200 items ran 200 extra queries - and the new whole-catalogue pickers made that worse. `VGC_SM_Repository::boms_by_output()` reads every recipe header once and caches it request-scoped (`self::$bom_map`), `save_bom()` clears it, and `get_bom()` is deliberately left uncached for the write paths that need the database as it is right now.
- **Hand-logged material use (1.89.0)**: material stock that was really used but NOT through a recipe. POST `/movements` accepts `type:'use'`, which maps to **`consume`** with a negative delta - the SAME type a production run writes for its components - so the history renders identically and no report needs a new category. The only difference is `ref_type` is EMPTY, which is exactly what makes it editable (`can_edit`) and undoable (`can_undo`); costing is untouched because `receipts()` reads `type='receive'` only. UI: `itemPage()` shows a `Log use` button (`data-act="use"`) for materials only (`!for_sale` - a product leaves by sale/shipment); `movementCard` adds the `use` title/label pair, asks no cost, keeps the date field, and labels the note "Used for ..." (optional - scrap is the one that demands a reason). `bindEditMovement` picks the form by TYPE first (`consume` -> use) and only then by sign, or a logged use would re-open labelled as a scrap.
- **QR colour (1.88.5)**: `qrSvg()` defaulted `fg` to `currentColor`, and `.vgc-sm-lbl` sets `color` to WHITE on a dark `label_color` (the labelInk luminance flip) - so the QR painted white on its own white tile and vanished. A QR is machine-read, so it must never inherit text colour: the default is now `#000000`, and labelSheet passes `fg:'#000000', bg:'#FFFFFF'` explicitly. The `bg` rect covers the quiet zone INSIDE the svg, which also fixes the Download-SVG path (it extracts the bare <svg>, previously with no background and a currentColor fill). Verified black-on-white across all 10 swatches and by round-trip decoding the matrix with zbar.
- **Per-side category shortcuts + item highlight (1.88.4)**: the sidebar renders a collapsible shortcut list under BOTH Products and Materials (`vgc-sm-catnav-{side}` / `vgc-sm-cattoggle-{side}`), each fetching its own pool and linking to its own list; open state is per side in localStorage (`vgc_sm_catnav_{side}`). `getCategories(scope, force)` caches per scope (`CATS_CACHE` is now a map) - callers passing nothing get 'product' (shop.js relies on that). `activeKey()` cannot tell a product from a material by path, so the screen that loads an item calls `V.setItemSide('product'|'material')`, which re-renders the chrome only when the side actually changes; `/item*` and `/recipe*` then highlight the correct list.
- **Calculated vs manual cost (1.88.3)**: the edit form used to POST `cost_net` from the input unconditionally, so opening+saving an item STAMPED the currently-shown calculated figure into storage and froze it. 1.88.1 made this visible for materials (the derived cost got written back as a typed one). Now: `shape_item` exposes `cost_derived` (the purchase figure, computed ALWAYS - even when a manual cost is set - so the form can offer the switch either way; stripped for viewers), the `cost_manual` toggle is shown whenever `has_bom || cost_derived != null` (was recipe-only), and **on save, leaving it calculated posts `cost_net: ''`** which `sanitize_item` maps to NULL - keeping derivation alive. `currentCost()` (margin baseline) follows the same rule. Rule to preserve: never write a derived/computed figure into `cost_net`; that column means "what a human typed".
- **Editable SKU (1.88.2)**: `sanitize_item()` deliberately has NO `sku` case (creation sets it once), which meant `update_item()` silently dropped a renamed SKU even though `item_input()` passed it through - the form appeared to save and nothing changed. `update_item()` now handles `sku` itself: trims, rejects empty, and rejects a clash with a named WP_Error (409) rather than letting the UNIQUE index swallow it; it therefore returns `bool|WP_Error` and the REST handler surfaces that. The other three callers (import, production kind-flip, shop pull) pass no `sku` key, so none can hit the new error path. The SKU is what a QR label encodes, so the form warns - only once the value actually differs - and the audit log records `SKU old -> new`.
- **Derived cost fallback (1.88.1)**: a material with NO typed cost is not free. `shape_item()` falls back to `avg_cost` (then `last_cost`) when there is no recipe, no manual override and nothing typed, and flags `cost_computed` so the list shows its calc pill. `effective_cost()` does the same before honouring the basis: 'manual' means PREFER what was typed, not 'prefer nothing' - returning 0.0 there was silently costing recipes at zero. Precedence is unchanged otherwise: cost_manual > typed (a typed 0 is a real answer) > derived > 0. 31 unit cases.
- **Scoped categories + backdating (1.88.0, DB 0.30.0)**: `categories.scope` is 'product'|'material'; name uniqueness is now PER SCOPE (create_category($name,$scope)). `get_categories($scope)`, GET `/categories?scope=`. One-time `migrate_category_scopes()` assigns by ACTUAL USAGE - material-only -> material, everything else -> product, and a category used by BOTH is duplicated (materials repointed to a same-named twin) so no item is orphaned and no new 'both' concept is needed. UI: the item form and quick-add filter by what the item IS and re-render the picker when the product/material select flips; the catalogue filter and the sidebar shortcuts (which hang off Products) request scope explicitly; the Categories admin renders two pools. **`viewItemForm` hoists `cats`/`item` to the function scope** - the category helpers live in the function body, not inside the fetch `.then`, and referencing the inner `var cats` threw 'cats is not defined'.
- **Backdating (1.88.0)**: `entry_datetime()` (trait-items, private but reachable from trait-admin via the composed class) validates YYYY-MM-DD, REFUSES a future date, and maps today->now / past->23:59:59 so a backfill sorts after same-day entries. Passed as `meta['created_at']` to record_movement, and as `created_at` through edit_movement. The UI only sends `date` when it differs from today.
- **prompt()/confirm() removed** from the category add (item form + quick-add) and category delete - they are banned project-wide and were silently dead in the PWA. Inline `catAddRow()` and an inline delete confirm replace them.
- **Editable stock entries (1.87.0, DB 0.29.0)**: movements gained `pack_qty`/`pack_size` (how a delivery was TYPED - frozen at entry so a later pack-size change cannot rewrite history; the box price is DERIVED as unit_cost*pack_size, never stored twice) and `edited_at`. Before this, packs were glued into the note as text ("2 box") and the box price was lost - do not reintroduce that. `VGC_SM_Corrections::can_edit()` is STRICTER than can_undo(): only a pure hand entry (ref_type empty, not cancelled, not a reversal) is editable; anything with a ref_type belongs to a document and routes there. `edit_movement()` revises in place, then `recompute_stock()` (ledger is truth, so rebuild rather than delta) + `Costing::recalculate()` when qty/cost moved, and logs before->after via `describe_changes()` as the audit trail. Route PUT/PATCH `/movements/{id}` at operator level (fixing your own typo is everyday work); Undo stays admin+correction-mode. The edit uses rawFetch, never the offline queue - revising history must be seen to succeed. `movementForm(item, type, entry)` serves both create and edit; in edit mode it prefills IN PACKS when the entry was packed.
- **Receipt refund-in-place (1.85.0)**: the old duplicate refund table is gone; `receiptLines(s, refundMode)` renders ONE table that grows a Refund-qty column in refund mode (manager, `s.refundable`, not voided). viewSale splits into fetch + `renderSale()`; module state `refundState={mode,voidConfirm}` drives a re-render from the cached sale (no refetch to toggle). Void is a two-step inline confirm (`voidConfirm`), NOT confirm() - browser dialogs are banned in this PWA (they silently no-op); the old bindRefund used confirm() and was fixed here. A voided sale reads as void: `.vgc-sm-void` banner + muted header card + struck total. Print moved to a page-level `receipt-print` bar. devids: `receipt-print`, `receipt-void`.
- **Sales chart (1.84.0)**: `class-sales.php recent()` adds a `series` (GROUP BY DATE, unbounded by the 500-row receipts cap) alongside rows/totals. Both halves moved into core in 1.166.0 when the statistics section needed the same picture: `V.buildBuckets( series, range, opts )` is the transform (UTC throughout; gap-fills the calendar; rolls up day->week->month at 92/550-day thresholds; `opts.value` names the plotted field) and `V.barChart( c, opts )` is pure render (HTML/CSS bars, not SVG - responsive with no scaling distortion; hover = native title + CSS highlight; single series so no legend, no palette validator). Plots net-of-refunds. devid `sales-chart`, hidden on empty range. When a chart is single-series magnitude, this is the pattern to copy.
- **Nav grouping (1.83.0)**: one model `NAV_GROUPS` (Catalogue/Trade/Sell/Stock/Admin) drives BOTH the desktop sidebar and the phone drawer; `itemVisible(n)` gates each item by `op`/`mgr`/`admin` and empty groups self-hide, so viewer-scope composes for free. Help is ungrouped, pinned bottom. Phone bottom bar is a FIXED five (Home, Products, Sell, Scan, Menu) - Sell -> `boot.posUrl` (till) for operator+, else `#/shop`; Menu opens the drawer. Reconciliations from the raw design: Material prices->Admin (postdates design, manager-only), Team stays a Settings sub-tab, Pull-from-shop dropped (no standalone route). When adding a destination: put it in a group with its permission flag - all three render sites follow.
- **Viewer scope (1.82.0, decided)**: a viewer sees stock quantities and locations, NOTHING with money. Enforced in three layers that must stay in step: (1) 14 GET routes bumped $auth→$write (partners+statement, notes, consignment/outstanding, orders, purchases, reports/*, production preview+plan); (2) `shape_item()` NULLs every money field (cost_*, avg/last_cost, margins, price_b2b) below operator — one gate covers lists, dashboard and single item; `cost_stats` and movement `unit_cost` likewise; (3) nav entries carry `op:1` and `navVisible()` filters all three render sites (sidebar, phone drawer, bottom tabs). Retail price_net stays visible (public anyway). When adding a screen with money on it: gate the route, strip the shape, flag the nav entry.

### Corrections — undoing entries made by mistake (1.80.0, DB 0.28.0)
`class-corrections.php`. The ledger is append-only, so a mistake is CANCELLED, never erased: an equal-and-opposite entry, linked both ways by `reverses` (on the cancelling row) and `reversed_at` (stamped on the row it cancels). Both columns exist on `movements` AND `location_ledger`. This generalises the pre-existing `VGC_SM_Production::void_run()`, which had been written but never wired to a route or a button — the 1.80.0 work is partly connecting it.

**One entry point.** All three undoable things write a movement on the item, so the item's movement history is the only UI: `scope_of()` dispatches on `ref_type` — `production_run` → the whole run via `void_run()` (never one leg), `location` → `pull()`/`push()` inverse (their guards apply, so undoing a push fails if the location already sold it), anything else → a plain contra entry. `legs_of()` expands a run to every leg so the preview shows the full consequence.

**`can_undo()` is the guard and is unit-tested (15 cases).** Refuses anything with its own undo route (`purchase`, `sale`, `sale_return`, `note`, `order`, `shop`, `production_void`) with a message naming where to go instead; refuses double-undo (`reversed_at` set) and undoing a cancellation (`reverses` set). Precedence: reversal > already-cancelled > guarded ref.

**Deliberate non-guard:** undoing a receipt whose stock is spent drives stock negative. That is ALLOWED and warned about, because it is exactly how "typed 100 instead of 10" is fixed — blocking it would make the mistake permanent.

Mode is per-user (`vgc_sm_fix_mode` user meta), admin-gated in the boot payload (`fixMode`) AND on every route, so an operator cannot reveal the controls. Cancelled receipts drop out of `VGC_SM_Costing::receipts()` via `reversed_at IS NULL`.

### Clearing test data (1.79.0)
`class-reset.php` splits every table into **transactional** (20, truncated) and **definitions** (kept). The line is "what happened" vs "what things are": movements/runs/purchases/orders/consignment/notes/sales/location ledgers/sync log/audit go; items, recipes, categories, partners, sale locations and their child rows stay. TRUNCATE not DELETE, so receipt and order numbering restarts at 1. Afterwards `items.stock_qty`/`store_qty` are zeroed and `avg_cost`/`last_cost`/`last_cost_at` NULLed — stock is the sum of a ledger that is now empty, and the derived costs came from purchase history that no longer exists. The reset is logged *after* truncation so it survives as entry #1 of the fresh log. GET `/reset/preview` returns the counts; POST `/reset` refuses without `confirm=RESET` (guards against a stale queued offline request or a double-tap). Both admin-only. **Adding a table means adding it to `transaction_tables()` or `kept_tables()` AND to `uninstall.php`** — a Python cross-check against the table helpers in the bootstrap catches drift in all three.

**When adding a setting, three places must agree**: the default + sanitiser in `class-sync-client.php`, AND the forward-list in `trait-shop.php::save_settings()`, AND the response in `get_settings()`. `cost_basis` shipped in 1.77.0 missing the last two, so the UI saved into a void — a browser harness that stubs the endpoint cannot catch this.

### Design system (0.16.0, implemented from a Claude Design project)
Warm parchment + terracotta, "Workshop backoffice".
Brand `#B65C22` · bg `#F5F1EA` · card `#FFF` · ink `#221F1A` · danger `#B42318` · ok `#2E7048` · dark payoff panel `#221F1A`.
Inline SVG icon set (`ICON_PATHS` / `icon()`). **Desktop ≥900px** = sidebar + tables + sticky cost panel; **phone** = bottom tabs + FAB. Numbers/SKUs/money are monospace + tabular.

### i18n (self-contained — the SPA has no `wp.i18n`)
English source strings are the **keys**. `VGC_SM_I18n::map()` = shipped catalogue **+ user overrides on top**. In JS: `t()` for composed/dynamic strings, toasts, prompts; plus `translateTree()` (a TreeWalker) that translates whole static text nodes and `placeholder`/`title`/`aria-label` after each render. **Changing language reloads the page** (the catalogue ships in the boot payload).
To add a language: add a catalogue method in `class-i18n.php` and list it in `languages()`.

---

## 7. Working conventions (follow these)

1. **Bump the version on every change** — plugin header + `VGC_STOCK_MANAGER_VERSION` constant + `readme.txt` Stable tag + changelog entry.
2. **Build zips with forward slashes.** PowerShell's `Compress-Archive` writes backslash entry paths that **break on Linux**. Use `System.IO.Compression.ZipArchive` and `CreateEntry(rel.Replace('\\','/'))`. Zips go to `_releases/` as `vgc-<slug>-<version>.zip`.
3. **The user tests on staging** — do not run or lint the plugin locally. (`node --check app/app.js` and a CSS brace check are fine and worth doing.)
4. **TEST MODE: publish every update immediately** (until the user says production mode). Publishing =
   - `gh release create vgc-<slug>-<version> _releases/<zip> --repo vgc-ltd-wp/vgc-plugin-updates`
   - update `plugins.json` in that repo (`version`, `download_url`, **`sha256` of the zip** — the updater refuses a mismatched package, prepend changelog)
   - **verify it on STAGING before calling it done** — `ssh vgc-sm-staging` (SiteGround, `~/www/vasilg18.sg-host.com/public_html`, WP-CLI, **PHP 8.2 / MySQL 8.4** against the harnesses' 7.4). Install the release zip with `wp plugin install <url> --force`, clear `wp-content/debug.log`, exercise what changed, read the log. Standing instruction from the owner, 2026-08-24. It has already caught: purchases tables never created on a fresh install, `AS lines` reserved on MySQL 8, an early-i18n notice on every request, and a schema fix that was a no-op for want of a DB_VERSION bump — none of which any local harness could see.
   - **update THIS file** — the "Pinned to" line, the feature-history table, and any section the change touches — and copy it to `docs/vgc-stock-manager-reference.md` in the same commit
   - commit, push, verify with `gh api .../contents/plugins.json` (the raw CDN lags ~5 min).
5. **This reference is always pinned to the current version.** It is part of the release, not an afterthought: a release is not finished until the doc matches what shipped. A stale reference is worse than none, because the next session will trust it.
6. **Self-update wiring**: instantiate `VGC_Plugin_Updater` **after** the `ABSPATH` guard. (Several older VGC plugins have it *inside* the guard, so it never runs — a real bug worth auditing separately.)
7. Update the memory files under `.claude/projects/.../memory/` when a durable decision changes.

Partner editor location rows gain a contact block; directory card shows each location's contact. DB_VERSION 0.10.0 (dbDelta adds the columns; no data migration). |

| **1.63.0** | **POS Phase 2 — the till (separate page) + checkout.** No DB change (tables from 1.62.0). **`class-sales::checkout($location_id, $lines, $discount_type, $discount_value, $note)`:** validates location active + per-line on-hand (oversell → 400); prices are gross; per-line discount ('pct'|'val') → `line_gross`; subtotal = Σ line_gross; cart discount on subtotal → `total_gross = subtotal − cart_discount`; the cart discount is **allocated across lines by gross share, last line takes the rounding remainder** so Σ final_gross == total_gross exactly, then each line's net = gross/(1+vat/100), vat = gross−net (per-line exact → totals exact). Writes `sales` (number `S-%05d` set post-insert) + `sale_lines`, calls `VGC_SM_Locations::deduct_for_sale` (location-only, no stock movement — goods left workshop at push), audits. `get($id)` shapes the receipt. **REST** POST `/sales` ($write) → `create_sale`. **Separate till page:** `class-frontend::maybe_render()` branches on `?vgc_sm_pos=1` (operator+, else redirect) → `render_pos()`: own `<head>`+`<body class="vgc-sm--pos">` with `#vgc-sm-pos`+`#vgc-sm-toast`, boot (restUrl/nonce/currency/strings/lang/user/perms), loads `pos_scripts()` = core.js + `js/pos/pos.tpl.js` + `js/pos/pos.js`, then `window.VGCSM_POS.start()` — **reuses core.js helpers (get/rawFetch/money/t/toast/translateTree) WITHOUT `VGCSM.start()`** (no backoffice router/chrome/SW-register). `posUrl` added to render_app boot. **Till JS** (`window.VGCSM_POS`, standalone): loads active locations → inventory grid (on-hand>0, client search filter) → tap-to-cart (qty capped at on-hand) → cart lines (qty stepper, editable gross price, per-line discount control, remove) + cart discount + live net/VAT/gross (math mirrors the server) → checkout POST `/sales` → receipt card (number+totals) + "New sale", inventory refetched. **Launcher:** "Open till" `<a target=_blank>` (boot.posUrl) on the `locations-toolbar`. CSS `.vgc-sm-pos*` (bar/grid/tiles/lines/totals; single-column ≤899px). Harness (till shell, real math): shell (active-only location select, back-to-app, 2 tiles, empty cart), add-to-cart, subtotal 39/net 32.50/vat 6.50, oversell cap at 6 + toast, line 10%→21.60, cart 10%→discounts 3.90/net 29.25/vat 5.85/total 35.10 (hand-verified), checkout POST body correct→receipt→inventory refetch→New-sale resets, phone single-column no-h-scroll, console clean. Server `php -l` clean (checkout/DB verifiable on staging). **Phase 3 next:** sales history + inventory/value views + CSV. |
| **1.62.0** | **POS Phase 1 — Sale locations (foundation). DB_VERSION 0.22.0.** New feature: multi-location retail. **Model chosen (user):** generalize the single physical store into named sale locations; **hard transfer** on push (goods leave workshop stock the moment they're pushed — `stock_qty` drops, location holds them; a sale later empties only the location). Company inventory = `stock_qty` + Σ`location_stock`. **Tables (5):** `sale_locations`, `location_ledger` (truth) + `location_stock` (cache, PK location+item, upserted via INSERT…ON DUPLICATE KEY), `sales`+`sale_lines` (created now, used by the till in Phase 2). **`class-locations.php`:** CRUD; `push()` = `record_movement(item,'to_location',-qty)` + ledger +qty (clamped to workshop `stock_qty`); `pull()` = reverse; `deduct_for_sale()` (Phase 2, location-only, no stock movement); on_hand/inventory/item_breakdown; `shape()` aggregates lines/units/retail/cost. **`class-sales.php`** stub. **Migration** `migrate_store()` (option `vgc_sm_pos_store_migrated`): creates a default "<site> — Store" location, hard-transfers each item's `store_qty` into it (movement −store_qty so stock_qty drops, ledger +store_qty), zeroes store_qty — "at workshop"/"at store" preserved, conservation holds. **REST** `trait-pos.php`: GET/POST `/locations`, PUT `/locations/{id}`, GET `/locations/{id}/inventory`, POST `/locations/{id}/push|pull` (all $mgr except reads $auth), GET `/items/{id}/locations`. **Backoffice JS** new `locations` domain (tpl+logic): `#/locations` list + `#/location/{id}` detail (inventory table, push-search→prompt→POST, pull-per-row, edit form); NAV_MANAGER "Sale locations", TITLES/activeKey/routeTitle wired; `app_scripts()` +2 files. **Item page:** the old `item-store` distribute/sold-at-store card removed (superseded); replaced by a read-only `item-locations` breakdown fetched from `/items/{id}/locations`; catalogue.js viewItem now Promise.all's a 3rd fetch. Devids documented. **Verified (frontend harness, manager perms):** nav link, list (2 rows, inactive badge, retail value), create→`#/location/3`, detail header/inventory, push (search→workshop-avail→prompt→POST `/push`), pull (POST `/pull`), edit (prefill+active→PUT), item-locations card present & store card gone, 22-route regression, console clean. Server (tables/migration/REST) is `php -l` clean; DB migration verifiable only on a real WP/DB (staging). **Deferred to Phase 2:** the separate till page (`?vgc_sm_pos=1`, own shell reusing core helpers, no backoffice router) + `class-sales` checkout + "Open till" launcher. Phase 3: sales history + inventory/value views. |
| **1.61.1** | **Fix: i18n drawer source labels were localised.** Root cause (not a capture bug — capture was correct English): `openDrawer()` runs `translateTree()` over the drawer, which localises any text node matching a catalogue key — so each English `<code>` source label got translated into the current language (all tokens read Cyrillic in bg). Fix in `bindI18n()`: after the drawer renders, restore every `.vgc-sm-i18nrow__src` textContent from its raw `data-i18ncopy` token (attributes are untouched by translateTree). The editable input keeps the current translation (STR[k]); copy/edit/save/filter already keyed off the English source attribute, unaffected. Verified: bg drawer labels now English ("Materials"/"Archived"/"Administrator"/"All categories") with Bulgarian inputs, 0/20 Cyrillic labels, filter on English keys, save still POSTs `strings.<EnglishKey>` + live re-render, console clean. |
| **1.61.0** | **Dev-mode i18n token inspector (core.js only).** Captures the exact translation tokens rendered on a page and lets you fix wording inline. **Capture:** `t(s)` and `translateTree()` push `s` into `_i18nSeen` while `_i18nRec` is on; `_i18nRec` tied to dev mode in `applyDevMode()` (zero cost off); reset per navigation at the top of `router()` — so the set = this page's tokens (union across in-page re-renders). **Launcher:** `ensureI18nLauncher(on)` adds/removes a fixed `#vgc-sm-i18nlaunch` "🌐 i18n" pill (dev mode only). **Drawer** (`openI18nDrawer`, reuses `openDrawer`): pauses recording while building; snapshots `_i18nSeen`; lazy-fetches `/translations?lang=` once into `_i18nCat` (base/value/overridden) → per-token state badge translated/edited(over)/missing via `i18nState()`; **completeness scan** walks APP text nodes + placeholder/title/aria-label for catalogue keys present as literal English but NOT in the token set → flagged "not via t()" (catches unwrapped strings). Rows: click `<code>` to copy token; inline `<input>` per token. **Save** (`bindI18n`) batches edits → `POST /translations {lang, strings}` → applies live (`STR[k]=val`, `HAS_STR=true`) → `closeDrawer()` + `router()` re-render so the new wording shows immediately; reopen shows updated state. English (`boot.lang==='en'`): read-only list (source has nothing to translate); degrades gracefully if `/translations` GET is forbidden (no scan/flags, list still works). CSS: `.vgc-sm-i18nlaunch` (bottom-left, clears bottom nav on mobile, print-hidden) + `.vgc-sm-i18nrow*`. No devids (dev tool). Harness (bg + catalogue): launcher present, 21 tokens on /items, states exact (Low=translated, Archived/Search=edited, All categories=missing), scan caught an injected unwrapped "Reports", filter, edit Low→POST {lang:bg,strings:{Low:…}}→live re-render (old gone, new shown)→reopen edited; dev-off removes launcher; en read-only + note; 19/20 routes clean (item/1 = harness fixture gap); console clean. |
| **1.60.0** | **Template/logic split — orders domain. REFACTOR COMPLETE: all 7 domains split (shop, consignment, partners, purchases, admin, catalogue, orders).** `tpl/orders.tpl.js` (12 fns → `V.tpl.orders`): `listPage`, `noPartners`, `orderTiles(tt)` (initial render + every `refresh()`), **`lineRow(l, idx, stillOut)`** (normal vs display-piece branch; stillOut passed in), `salesCard(items)` (report rows prepared by logic — title/sub/paid), `paymentsCard({payments, balance, today})`, **`docPage(o)`** (o.doc null for new; termsValue resolved by logic; normal/display line groups prebuilt; salesHtml/paymentsHtml composed in), `searchResults`, **`saleRow(l, {avail, price, qty, amount})`** + **`saleDrawer`** (all availability/draft math in logic), **`printSheet(o)`** (rows arrive as {name, sku, qty, unitPrice, vat, lineTotal} — billable×netUnit math in logic) + `printBar`. `orders.js` logic-only (~560 ln): st/lines/payments/saleReports state, billable/stripVat/netUnit/netCost/stillOutOf/totals (mirrors `VGC_SM_Orders::strip_vat`), syncForm, refresh, sale-draft state machine (avail caps, auto-price vs manual-amount detection), print-data assembly, save/complete/payments/sale-report endpoints, print-overlay lifecycle. Core ends by building `window.VGCSM` (`V`): exports every core function + `boot`/`APP`, `V.register(obj)` merges into `V.screens`, and `V.start()` holds the old boot block (barcode-prefill wrapper now wraps `S.viewItemForm`, global search uses `S.itemsState`/`S.loadItems`, SW registration, `flushQueue()`, `router()`). **screens-legacy.js** (~5,190 ln): all screens verbatim inside its own IIFE — preamble destructures 51 helpers off `V` (`var esc = V.esc; …`), tail registers 27 view fns + `stopScanner`/`loadItems`/`itemsState`/`openItemQuickAdd`/`partnerForm`/`movementForm`. **PHP:** `Frontend::app_scripts()` returns the ordered file list; render_app loops `<script>` tags + `<script>window.VGCSM.start();</script>`; SW precache built from the same list (add a file in ONE place). **Tooling:** codemap + components.py walk `app/js/*.js`. Verified in a served harness: all 34 routes error-free, dev-mode badges (incl. after partner-tab swaps), purchase quick-add drawer w/ categories, console clean. Screen files must never declare a top-level name that collides with a core export (var-over-function hoisting would silently shadow it). |
| **1.50.0** | **Logout fix — service worker was swallowing the auth redirect.** The Sign out link is a plain `<a href=boot.logoutUrl>` (nonce'd `?vgc_sm_logout=1`); the server handler in `maybe_render()` is correct. The bug was client-side: the PWA service worker's `navigate` branch does `fetch(req)`, which follows the server's 302 and returns a `redirected:true` response — which the browser refuses to use for a navigation, so `respondWith` errors and the click does nothing. Fix in `output_service_worker()` (`class-frontend.php`): the fetch handler now parses the URL up front and, for any request whose query has `vgc_sm_logout` or `vgc_sm_login`, `return`s without `respondWith` so auth flows perform a normal, unmediated browser navigation to the server. SW re-installs automatically on the version bump (cache name is `vgc-sm-<VERSION>`); users must reload once after updating for the new worker to activate. Server-generated SW → not exercisable in the JS harness; verified by PHP lint + reasoning. |
| **1.49.0** | **Partner page → compact tabbed layout (design v2 `scPartner`).** `viewPartner` rewritten: fetch once, then a fixed **header** (`partner-header`) + **action bar** (`partner-actions`, incl. Edit) + **tab strip** (`partner-tabs`) over a single `#pa-body` that swaps client-side (`renderTab()` → `bodyHtml()` + `bindBody()` + `decorateDevMode()`), **no re-fetch on tab switch** (important — the partner fetch now computes turnover). Tabs: **Overview** (`partner-summary` tiles + `partner-recent` = latest orders/purchases/notes merged, newest first, 6), **Orders** (`partner-orders`), **Purchases** (`partner-purchases`), **Consignment** (`partner-outstanding`+`partner-held`+`partner-notes`), **Prices** (`partner-prices`, search-to-add + inline edit/del bound only on this tab), **Details** (`partner-directory`). Edit-partner stays in the fixed action bar → `partnerForm(p)` mounts into a fixed `#pa-form`. New CSS: `button.vgc-sm-subtab` reset + `.vgc-sm-subtabs--scroll` (nowrap/overflow-x). All prior partner devids preserved (relocated into tabs) + added `partner-tabs`, `partner-recent`. Verified in a harness: 6 tabs, correct per-tab content, **fetch count stayed 1** across all switches, prices search/edit + edit-partner form + row clicks work, dev badges reapply per tab, mobile no h-scroll, console clean. |
| **1.48.0** | **Partners list — turnover per row (financial always visible).** A settled partner (owed=0, we_owe=0) showed no money on its row; now every traded partner shows **total value traded** as the headline. `shape_partner` adds `orders_value` (`VGC_SM_Orders::turnover_for_partner`) + `purchases_value` (`VGC_SM_Purchases::spend_for_partner`) — each sums `totals()['gross']` over the partner's orders/purchases (all statuses, limit 500). Row `.vgc-sm-partrow__bal` now: `.vgc-sm-partrow__turnover` = `money(orders_value+purchases_value)` + a small uppercase "traded" label (shown when >0), then `owes €X`/`you owe €X` beneath when non-zero. `.is-owes` softened to `--vgc-ink-2`, amt to 12.5px (secondary to turnover). **Perf note:** turnover loads each of the partner's orders/purchases (via `get()`+`totals()`) — fine at workshop scale (few partners); if partner/doc counts grow, cache order/purchase gross or aggregate in SQL. Verified in a harness (settled customer → "€2,340.50 traded" only; owing customer → turnover + owes; supplier → turnover + you-owe; no-trade → nothing). |

Row avatar bumped to **42px** (`.vgc-sm-partrow .vgc-sm-ava { width/height:42px; font-size:16px }`; base `.vgc-sm-ava` stays 34px for team/audit). Balance switched to the design's inline format: `owes €X` (ink, `.is-owes` → `--vgc-ink`) / `you owe €X` (amber `.is-youowe` → `--vgc-brand-dark` #8A4417), dropping the stacked `<small>` labels; both lines show for a dual partner. Row `padding:13px 4px; min-height:66px` per design. The 1.46.0 trade counts (`N orders · M purchases · contact`) remain as the meta sub-line (an addition to the design, per the user's earlier request). Verified in a harness. |

New CSS `.vgc-sm-partrow*` gives uniform, roomy rows (fixes the uneven spacing from the old `<br>`-in-`.vgc-sm-qty` layout). **Server:** `shape_partner` now returns `order_count`/`purchase_count` from new `VGC_SM_Orders::count_for_partner()` / `VGC_SM_Purchases::count_for_partner()` (single `COUNT(*)` each — cheap; the list already does the heavier `balance_owed`). | **1.45.0** | **Dedicated order print template (design v2 orderPrint).** Replaced print-by-reshaping-the-live-screen with a purpose-built document. `openOrderPrint()` (inside `viewOrder`, hoisted; the `#o-print` button calls it) builds an invoice-style `.vgc-sm-psheet` and shows it in a full-screen `.vgc-sm-printov` overlay (Close + Print/Save-PDF, both `.vgc-sm-noprint`). Sheet = letterhead (`boot.print.name/address/vat` + `◐` mark) · ORDER/number/dates/status · Billed-to (partner, first location, terms, mode) / Payment (terms, transport, note) · line table `#/Item/Qty/Unit price/VAT/Line total` using **billable qty × netUnit** (matches the order's own `totals()`) · display-pieces-on-loan note · Net/VAT/Total/Paid/Balance (green paid, red balance, from `tt`) · footer (`boot.print.footer` + IBAN). Fixed light palette (theme-independent). **Print isolation:** body gets `.vgc-sm-has-printov` while open; a dedicated `@media print` block hides `.vgc-sm-shell`/nav/toast/printbar and prints only the sheet full-width. **Server:** `VGC_SM_Sync::settings()` gained `biz_name/biz_address/biz_vat/biz_iban/print_footer`; `save_settings` guards `auto_push` (keep-current when absent); `trait-shop` `save_settings` only forwards **present** params (so a partial save can't blank the connection) and `get_settings` returns the biz fields; `render_app` boot exposes `print{name(fallback siteName)/address/vat/iban/footer}`. **SPA:** new Settings **Printed documents** card (`settings-print`, own Save → POST /settings biz-only → updates `boot.print` live). Verified in a harness (sheet numbers Net120/VAT24/Total156-incl-transport/Paid100/Balance56, billable qty 10 & 4-after-return, display note, letterhead/footer/IBAN; print-emulation hides shell+printbar, sheet full-width no-shadow; Close/Escape dismiss; console clean). Note: the old generic `@media print` (1.34.0, tiles etc.) still governs statement/partner printing. |
| **1.44.0** | **Fix: over-tall "Markup on all lines" input (purchase-lines).** `#p-defmarkup` is a `.vgc-sm-input` inside `.vgc-sm-toolbar` (`.vgc-sm-toolbar .vgc-sm-input { flex: 1 1 200px }`) **and** wrapped in a `.vgc-sm-of` (a *column* flex) — so the `200px` flex-basis applied to the item's main axis = **height**, rendering it ~200px tall. Pinned the input inline: `width:90px;height:34px;flex:none` (the `flex:none` stops the vertical stretch; resolves to the standard 44px control height). **Gotcha for later:** don't drop a bare `.vgc-sm-input` into a `.vgc-sm-toolbar` when it's also inside a `.vgc-sm-of` column — the toolbar's `flex:1 1 200px` becomes a height. Verified in a harness (height matches `#p-mode` = 44px, width 90px, console clean). |
| **1.43.0** | **Fix: categories in the purchase quick-add drawer.** `openItemQuickAdd` was called with `[]` cats from the purchase, so its category `<select>` was empty and had no add-new. Now it **loads categories itself** (`get('/categories')` when `cats` empty) and its select is built by a local `catSelectHtml(list,current)` that includes a `＋ Add new category…` (`__add__`) option, wired by `bindCat(list)` → prompt → `POST /categories` → rebuild select with `created_id` selected → `refreshCategories()` (global) → toast — mirroring the item form's `categorySelectHtml`/`bindCategorySelect`. Restructured into an inner `build(catList)` so the fetch can precede drawer open. Save guards `category_id: (val('qa-cat')==='__add__' ? '' : ...)`. Verified in a harness: drawer lists existing cats + add-new; add-new POSTs, rebuilds selected, toasts; item save carries the new `category_id`; console clean. |
| **1.42.0** | **Add-item flow: full page in catalogue, quick-add drawer on purchase (user request).** Reverted the catalogue `items-add` button + `.vgc-sm-fab` back to `linkBtn(...newHref)` plain links to `#/item/new` (the full item form, as pre-1.35.0); removed the `openItemQuickAdd` click-wiring from `viewItems`. The quick-add drawer now serves the **Purchase** screen: `wireQuickItem()` (the `#p-newitem` handler) opens `openItemQuickAdd([], 'products', {title, subtitle, onSaved})` and its `onSaved(it)` calls the purchase's `addLine(...)` — replacing the old inline `quickItemHtml()`/`#p-new-wrap` form (both removed). `openItemQuickAdd` generalised: `opts.onSaved(item)` (hands back the created item instead of `loadItems()`), `opts.title`/`opts.subtitle`, and the **Full-form link is hidden** when `onSaved` is set (navigating away would abandon the purchase). SKU is now **optional** (blank → server auto-generates), matching the old purchase quick-add; validation requires name only. **Bug fixed during the change:** a leftover `sku` variable reference (after relaxing validation) threw in the save handler and silently blocked the POST — now `val('qa-sku')`. Verified in a harness: catalogue Add/FAB are anchors to `#/item/new` (no drawer); purchase New-item opens the drawer (no full-form link, SKU optional), save POSTs the item and adds the line, drawer closes, console clean. No component-ID (`data-devid`) changes. |

| **1.38.0** | **Dev mode Phase 2 — trade & partner screen tags (tagging only).** Added `data-devid` to: Orders list (`orders-toolbar`, `orders-list`), Purchases list (`purchases-toolbar`, `purchases-list`), Partners list (`partners-toolbar`, `partners-list`), **Order** (`order-header` [docHeader wrapped], `order-summary`, `order-details`, `order-lines`, `order-onloan`, `order-sales` [in `salesCardHtml`], `order-payments`, `order-actions`), **Purchase** (`purchase-header`, `purchase-summary`, `purchase-details`, `purchase-lines`, `purchase-payments`, `purchase-actions`), **Statement** (`statement-filter`, `statement-tiles` [wraps `statTiles`], `statement-timeline`, `statement-actions`), **Partner** (`partner-header`, `partner-summary`, `partner-actions`, `partner-directory`, `partner-outstanding`, `partner-held`, `partner-orders`, `partner-purchases`, `partner-notes`, `partner-prices`) — **39 IDs / 8 pages**, all in `components.md`. **Gotcha fixed & noted for later phases:** `decorateDevMode()` appends the badge as a *child* of the tagged element, so tagging a container whose `innerHTML` is later replaced in place (here `#o-summary`/`#p-summary` via `refresh()`/`refreshTotals()`) **wipes the badge** → moved those two devids onto a non-re-rendered wrapper `<div data-devid=…><div id=…>tiles</div></div>`. **Rule for Phase 3+: never put `data-devid` directly on an element that gets `el.innerHTML = …` after render (e.g. catalogue `#items-list` via `renderItems`) — wrap it instead.** Verified in a harness: Order 6 badges (incl. order-summary after the fix), Partner 8, no errors, console clean. **Next: Phase 3** (catalogue, item form, recipe, produce, scan). |
| **1.37.0** | **Dev mode — clickable badge + comment popover.** Replaced the pure-CSS `::before` label (top-left, inert) with a **real clickable `.vgc-sm-devbadge`** at each panel's **top-right**, injected by `decorateDevMode()` which now runs at the end of `screen()` (every render) and inside `applyDevMode()`. Clicking a badge opens `openDevPop(badge,id)` — a fixed-position `.vgc-sm-devpop` (id title + `<textarea>` + Copy/Close via `btn()`), positioned under the badge and clamped to the viewport. **Copy** → `copyText('In `+'`'+`id`+'`'+`: '+note)` (empty note → just `` `id` ``) with a "paste it to the dev" toast; closes after. Dismiss on outside `mousedown` (capture) or Escape. Removed the old Alt+click id-copy (superseded). New CSS: `.vgc-sm-devbadge` (abs top:0/right:0, translateY(-100%), cursor pointer), `.vgc-sm-devpop(__id/__note/__foot)`; print-hides both. `data-devid` tags unchanged (still Item detail only). Verified in a harness: 5 right-side badges, click→popover, comment copy = `` In `item-header`: make the image larger ``, empty = `` `item-stock` ``, outside-click dismiss, badges removed when off (panels stay tagged), console clean. | **1.36.0** | **Dev mode Phase 1 — component-ID overlay (the mechanism).** A per-user aid: turn on *Developer mode* (Settings) and every tagged panel shows its **component ID** badge + dashed outline, so change requests can name the exact block. **Server:** `set_dev_mode()` in `trait-admin.php` + route `POST /me/dev-mode` (perm `$auth`, body `{on}`) → `update_user_meta(uid,'vgc_sm_dev_mode')`; `render_app()` boot gains `devMode => (bool) get_user_meta(...)`. **App (app.js):** `applyDevMode(on)` toggles `.vgc-sm-devmode` on `<body>`; `setDevMode(on)` also POSTs to persist; `applyDevMode(boot.devMode)` at init; a **capture-phase** document click handler copies `data-devid` on **Alt+click** (clipboard + bare-id toast fallback). Settings gains a *Developer mode* card (`#s-devmode`). **CSS:** `.vgc-sm-devmode [data-devid]` outline + `::before{content:attr(data-devid)}` badge (brand, mono, positioned above; nested tag → badge on the right); hidden in `@media print`. **Convention:** `page-component` (dash), lowercase, panels only. **Tags are always in the HTML (inert when off)** → toggling is instant, no re-render. Proof screen: **Item detail** — `item-header`, `item-stock`, `item-store`, `item-actions`, `item-movements`. **New repo docs:** `vgc-stock-manager-components.md` (the component map — page→component→id→description, with a Coverage checklist) and `vgc-stock-manager-components.py` (audit: lists ids grouped by page, flags any missing from the MD). Verified in a harness: 5 badges (correct ids/brand/dashed outline), class-toggle shows/hides instantly with attrs retained, Settings toggle POSTs `{on:0|1}` + flips class, Alt+click copies, badges gone when off, console clean. **Next: Phase 2** tags the trade/partner screens (Orders/Purchases lists, Order, Purchase, Statement, Partner, Partners) and fills in `components.md`. |
| **1.35.0** | **Redesign Phase 5 — item quick-add drawer (additive).** New `openItemQuickAdd(cats, scope)` (app.js, before item-detail): a slide-in via `openDrawer()` for fast item creation from the essentials — Name, SKU, `for_sale` (Product/Material, presets from the list `scope`), `kind` (raw/manufactured), Unit (static pcs/g/kg/ml/l), Category (from the catalogue's already-fetched `cats`), Unit cost, Retail price (hidden via `#qa-price-wrap` when Material), VAT%. Save → `write('/items','POST', body)` with the same field names as the full form (`is_sellable` mirrors `for_sale`, `reorder_level:0`, `store_stocked:0`), then `closeDrawer()` + `loadItems()`. name+SKU required. `viewItems`: the desktop **Add item** `linkBtn`→`btn(id="items-add")` opens the drawer; the mobile `.vgc-sm-fab` keeps `href=newHref` (no-JS fallback) but its click is intercepted (`preventDefault`) to open the drawer. **The rich full-page item form is untouched** and linked from the drawer footer (`Full form…` → `#/item/new`) — this is the user-approved *lightweight second create path*, NOT a replacement (they chose \"add a lightweight quick-add drawer\"). | **1.34.0** | **Redesign Phase 5 — print polish for design-v2 primitives (print-only CSS).** The `@media print` block (app.css ~line 1047) predated Phases 1–4, so it flattened the old `.vgc-sm-statrow`/`.vgc-sm-kpis` but **not** the new primitives — a printed order/statement/partner showed the dark **hero tile as a solid black box**, plus avatars/icons as noise. Added print rules (inside the existing `@media print`, single-tab indent): `.vgc-sm-tiles` (force `repeat(4,1fr)`, tight gap, break-inside avoid), `.vgc-sm-tile` (bordered white), `.vgc-sm-tile--hero` + children (override the dark-inverted bg/text to white/black; value 14pt), `.vgc-sm-tile--good/--bad .vgc-sm-tile__v` (darkened ink greens/reds `#1a6b3a`/`#a01810`), `.vgc-sm-dochead`/`.vgc-sm-parthead` (keep words, `__ava`/`.vgc-sm-ava`/`.vgc-sm-noteicon` → `display:none`), `.vgc-sm-onloan`/`.vgc-sm-notebanner` (flatten tints to bordered white), `.vgc-sm-drawerov` → `display:none` (never print an open overlay). **Print-only — screen appearance untouched.** No JS/DB change. Verified in a browser harness by flipping the `@media print` `CSSMediaRule.media.mediaText` to `'all'` (Chrome print emulation isn't exposed via the MCP): hero dark `rgb(16,14,12)`→white/black, tiles 4-col, good=`#1a6b3a`/bad=`#a01810`, `.vgc-sm-back` hidden, `.vgc-sm-printhead` shown; and critically **before** the flip the hero was still dark (proves the rules don't leak to screen); console clean. **Design decision:** the design's other Phase-5 thread — converting the rich full-page item form (`viewItem`/item form: recipe/BOM, images, dual pricing, store distribution, barcode) into the simplified `itemDrawer` — was **NOT** done: it's a large lateral rewrite that would regress a working screen (same principle as 3c line-grid / 4e tables). The slide-in drawer pattern already ships where it's genuinely additive (Phase 3b Record-a-sale). Flagged to the user as optional. |

New CSS `.vgc-sm-ava` (34px round, `--vgc-sunken` bg) — distinct from the 52px `.vgc-sm-parthead__ava`. `viewTranslations()` and `viewSettings()` were already clean forms/tables → left untouched (a table→list rewrite would regress, per the 4c principle).
