| **1.28.0** | **Redesign Phase 3c (light) — order/purchase line-grid polish (visual only).** User chose "light polish" over a full design-match after I flagged that the design's fixed-width horizontal-scroll grid would *regress* the current container-query responsive grid (which keeps all columns visible on desktop and self-labels each field when it wraps). So the responsive grid stays; only the look changes. app.css: `.vgc-sm-of__ro` and `.vgc-sm-oline/.vgc-sm-pline .vgc-sm-input[type="number"]` now use `font-family: var(--vgc-mono)` + right-align + tabular-nums (numbers read as aligned figures); new `.vgc-sm-of__m.is-good`/`.is-bad` colour the order Margin (green `--vgc-ok` / red `--vgc-danger`); the `@media (max-width:1099px)` blocks give each `.vgc-sm-oline`/`.vgc-sm-pline` a full border + radius + margin (per-line **cards** on phone), with `:first-child` border-top re-asserted (the base rule zeroes it). app.js: order `refresh()` toggles `is-good`/`is-bad` on the margin cell by sign. No markup/behaviour/DB change (still DB 0.21.0). Verified in a browser harness (order with a +margin and a −margin line): desktop shows green €7.00 / red −€6.00 margins, mono right-aligned inputs + read-only cells, lines stay flat (no card) with no h-scroll; mobile shows each line as a 10px-radius bordered card (incl. first), labels shown, no h-scroll; console clean. **Cache note:** the local http.server sends no-cache headers of its own, so the browser served stale app.css/js from the prior phase until asset URLs were given a `?v=` query — always cache-bust the harness after CSS/JS edits. |
| **1.27.0** | **Redesign Phase 3b — Record-a-sale UI on the Order screen.** Wires the 1.26.0 sale-report backend into `viewOrder()` (app.js). Local state gains `saleReports` from `o.sale_reports`. New helpers (hoisted in `viewOrder` scope): `isConsignment()` (=`payment_mode==='on_sale'`), `orderLineByItem`, `saleUnitPrice` (=line `unit_price` as typed), `saleAvail(itemId, report)` = `max(0, sent−returned−qty_sold)` **plus** the report's own line qty when editing (because `qty_sold` already aggregates every saved report), `saleLineSummary`, `salesCardHtml()`, and `openSaleDrawer(report)`. `salesCardHtml()` renders only when `o && isConsignment()` — a "Sales from sent stock" card (`.vgc-sm-salelogs`) with a Record-sale button and one tappable log row per report (edit) + a trash button (delete), inserted before the Payments card. `openSaleDrawer` builds a slide-in via `openDrawer()`: candidate rows (non-display, sent>0, avail>0 or already in the edited report) each with a `.vgc-sm-stepper` (capped at `saleAvail`) + a "Vendor pays" number input (auto = qty×unit_price unless the user typed a manual amount — tracked by comparing `round2(amount)` to the priced value), a Settlement block (actual paid + invoice), and a live footer (count + total, `#sale-due`/`#sale-total`/`#sale-count` patched in place, no re-render so focus survives). Save calls the order `save()` **first** (persist edits) then POST `/orders/{id}/sale-reports` (or PUT `/{rid}`), then swaps `o/lines/payments/saleReports` from the response and re-renders; delete confirms then DELETEs. New CSS (app.css, "Phase 3b" block): `.vgc-sm-drawer__title`, `.vgc-sm-salelog(s)`, `.vgc-sm-salerow(__name/__ctl)`, `.vgc-sm-saleamt`, `.vgc-sm-saleset(__grid)`, `.vgc-sm-salefoot(__tot)`; reuses the Phase 0 `.vgc-sm-stepper` + drawer head/body/foot. Verified in a browser harness (consignment order, one seeded report): card renders + gated off on pay-in-full; drawer lists the right candidates (display piece excluded, already-sold capped); steppers auto-price, cap at avail, manual override persists across a step; footer totals live; **add** saves order then POSTs the exact body (qty/amount/paid_actual/invoice/sold_on); **edit** prefills, avail adds own qty back, PUTs with rid; **delete** confirms + DELETEs; console clean. Line-grid cell restyle (`cellIn`/`cellRO`) + phone stacked line cards for Orders & Purchases still deferred (Phase 3c). |
| **1.26.0** | **Redesign Phase 3a — consignment sale-report backend (dormant; no UI).** Records the discrete "the vendor sold N of these" events on a consignment order. New tables `vgc_sm_order_sale_reports` (order_id, sold_on, invoice, paid_actual, total, payment_id) + `vgc_sm_order_sale_lines` (report_id, item_id, qty, amount); DB_VERSION **0.21.0** (added to `create_tables()` — no `--` comments; table helpers `vgc_sm_order_sale_reports_table()`/`vgc_sm_order_sale_lines_table()`). Model in `class-orders.php`: `sale_reports()`, `add_sale_report()`, `update_sale_report()`, `delete_sale_report()` (+ private `clean_sale_lines`/`apply_sold_deltas`/`sync_report_payment`). Design (delta-based, composes with manual edits): saving a report bumps each order line's `qty_sold` by the **delta** vs the report's prior lines, then calls `reconcile()`; `paid_actual` (defaults to the report total) is mirrored into an **order payment**, its id stored on the report and re-synced/removed on edit/delete. `avail` for a line = sent − returned − qty_sold (+ this report's own qty when editing). REST (in `trait-orders.php` + routes in `class-rest-api.php`): POST `/orders/{id}/sale-reports`, PUT/DELETE `/orders/{id}/sale-reports/{rid}`; `shape_order` now emits `sale_reports[]`. Verified with a **stubbed-`$wpdb` delta test** (24 assertions: add/update/delete qty_sold deltas, total, payment create/update/delete + linkback, reconcile ran, empty-lines rejected). No screen wired yet — the Record-a-sale drawer + per-order sales log land in **Phase 3b**. |
| **1.25.0** | **Redesign Phase 2 — Purchase document re-skin (visual only).** Mirror of Phase 1 on `viewPurchase()`: h1+completed tag → `docHeader(number, vendor, status, mode Bought/Consignment, href)`; the old `.vgc-sm-statrow` "Money" card (lower down) → a `statTiles` summary in `#p-summary` at the top (Cost·exVAT / VAT / We-owe-them-hero / Paid / Balance good-bad / Retail·exVAT / Potential-profit pct+profit / Still-held), re-rendered by `refreshTotals()`. New local `purchaseTiles(tt)`. The profit-assumption note moved under the tiles. All ids/`data-s`/`data-f` hooks and behaviour preserved. Verified in a browser harness: renders (hero We-owe €72.00 correct), editing qty_received re-renders summary (€132.00), Save body unchanged (line received=20), old statrow gone, console clean. Line grid (`.vgc-sm-pline`) cell restyle still deferred with Orders’ to after Phase 3. |
| 1.24.0 | **Redesign Phase 1 — Order document re-skin (visual only).** `viewOrder()` render rebuilt on the Phase 0 primitives, preserving every id/`data-*` hook and all behaviour (totals/save/reconcile/payments untouched). Changes: h1+status/overdue tags → `docHeader(number, partner, status, mode, href)`; the old `.vgc-sm-otot` Balance card → a `statTiles` summary in `#o-summary` at the top (Net/VAT/Total-hero/Paid/Balance/Margin+profit-qualifier/Still-out), re-rendered wholesale by `refresh()` so tone follows the numbers; display-pieces wrapped in a blue on-loan card (`.vgc-sm-onloan`, info-bg) — visual only, still inline-editable (mark-returned interaction deferred). New local `orderTiles(tt)`. Verified in a browser harness with a real order: renders, hero Total €57.36 correct, editing qty_sold re-renders the summary (€80.64) and per-line still-out/margin, Save PUT body unchanged (line sold=6, VAT flag, 3 lines), blue card holds the display line, payments/actions intact, console clean, no h-scroll. Deferred to Phase 1b: line-grid cell restyle (`cellIn`/`cellRO`) + phone stacked line cards. |
| 1.23.0 | **Redesign Phase 0 — foundations (additive, dormant).** Imported the Claude Design v2 artifact (`vgc-stock-manager-design-v2.html`, a JS prototype) and translated its primitives into app.js/app.css WITHOUT wiring them into any screen. Added: 6 icons (partners/orders/purchases/notes/consignOut/unit); the badge taxonomy on `.vgc-sm-tag` (roles filled, sourcing outlined, status semantic — helpers `badge`/`roleBadges`/`statusBadge`); unified summary `statTiles` (`.vgc-sm-tiles`/`.vgc-sm-tile`, hero=dark inverted, good/bad, mono values); `docHeader`; line cells `cellIn`/`cellRO`; and a slide-in `openDrawer`/`closeDrawer` (`.vgc-sm-drawerov`/`.vgc-sm-drawer`, closes on overlay/Escape/`[data-drawer-close]`, focus in+restore). **Collision averted:** `.vgc-sm-badge` was the nav dot, so pills use `.vgc-sm-tag`. Verified in a browser harness: all primitives render, taxonomy visually distinct, hero tile dark, drawer opens/focuses/closes 3 ways, console clean; live app unchanged (only --danger/ok/outline/product tag modifiers are used by screens today). Next: Phase 1 adopts these in the Order document. |
| 1.22.2 | **Fix: missing item columns — the real cause of "stuck as material / edits don't save".** The 1.21.0 `CREATE TABLE` carried inline `-- SQL comments`, which **dbDelta cannot parse**, so on existing installs `for_sale` (and likely `price_net`/`price_b2b`/`cost_manual`/`store_stocked`/`store_qty`) were never ADDed — reads returned empty (all items read as materials), writes to the column failed silently (role edits didn't persist). Removed all `--` comments from CREATE TABLE. Added idempotent `VGC_SM_Repository::ensure_item_columns()` (SHOW COLUMNS → ALTER ADD for each missing column) + `maybe_seed_for_sale()` (re-seeds only if nothing is for_sale yet, so it can't clobber edits). New bootstrap migration guarded by `vgc_sm_item_columns_v2`; DB_VERSION 0.20.1. Seed heuristic improved: now also marks `kind='manufactured'` as products (per user: most items are made-to-sell). Verified SQL generation against a stubbed `$wpdb` (all 6 missing columns ALTERed, correct seed UPDATE). **Lesson: never comment inside a CREATE TABLE dbDelta sees; the JS harness stubs the server so it cannot catch schema bugs.** |
| 1.22.1 | **Fix (1.21.0 bug, surfaced now): item read as both product AND material.** `tagHtml(kind)` still rendered the words "Product"/"Material", colliding with the new `for_sale` role badge — a made item seeded as a material showed both "Material" (role) and "Product" (kind), and a saved role change looked unsaved because the kind tag never moved. Relabelled the sourcing tag to **Made/Bought** (i18n `Made` added; `Bought` existed). The refactor (1.22.0) was **exonerated** first: a body-diff harness confirmed all 98 REST method bodies byte-identical, and a browser harness proved the role save round-trips (PUT sends `for_sale:1`, badge updates). Note: the 1.21.0 seed only marked sellable/priced items as products, so made-products with no price and not shop-sellable seeded as materials — users re-mark them individually (save works). |
| 1.22.0 | **Refactor (no behaviour change): `class-rest-api.php` split into resource traits.** The 3,212-line class became a ~620-line main class (route table `register_routes`, permission callbacks, `error_response`) that `use`s 9 traits under `includes/rest/` (`trait-items/production/partners/notes/orders/purchases/shop/reports/admin.php`), each holding one resource's handlers + `shape_*`. Traits are `require_once`d at the top of the main file. Safe because `self::`/`__CLASS__`/`array(__CLASS__,'x')` all resolve to the composed class regardless of the defining trait. **Verified:** the route-dump harness shows all 79 route-configs register byte-identically (same methods, callbacks, permission callbacks), and ReflectionClass confirms all 98 methods present, none extra, distributed 8 main + 90 across 9 traits. No DB change (0.20.0). To add an endpoint now: route in `register_routes()`, handler in the resource trait. |
| 1.21.0 | **Material/product divide: nav split + `for_sale` flag.** New items column `for_sale` (DB **0.20.0**), independent of `kind` (which stays bought/made and drives recipes/production). One-time `VGC_SM_Repository::seed_for_sale()` (guarded by option `vgc_sm_for_sale_seed`) sets for_sale=1 where `is_sellable=1 OR price_net IS NOT NULL OR price_b2b IS NOT NULL`. shape_item/item_input/sanitize_item + `list_items` gain `for_sale`; shop-pull and CSV import set it. Nav: `#/items` entry replaced by `#/products` + `#/materials` (new `beaker` icon); `#/items` kept as an unlisted all-view for back-compat; category subnav now hangs off `#/products`. `viewItems(scope)` (products/materials/all) drives title, the for_sale list filter, and a scope-aware New-product/New-material button (`#/item/new?for_sale=1|0`). Item form: a clear "This item is" role select (`f-for_sale`), the old Type relabelled "How it is obtained" (Bought in / Made from a recipe); item detail gains a Product/Material badge + a for_sale-aware back-link. **Bug caught in the browser harness before shipping:** the barcode-prefill wrapper `viewItemForm = function (id){ origItemForm(id) }` silently dropped the new `query` arg, so `?for_sale=` defaults failed — fixed to forward `(id, query)`. Verified end-to-end (nav, scoped lists, form defaults, edit) via a boot+fetch-stub harness; see memory [[verify-spa-ui-in-browser]]. |
| 1.20.1 | **Hotfix: item edit crashed with "i is not defined".** `bindPricing()` (a sibling helper defined OUTSIDE viewItemForm's `.then(function(r){ var i })` closure) referenced `i` via the new `currentCost()`. `node --check` passes (syntax-only) — the scope bug only shows at render. Fixed by threading the item in: `function bindPricing(i)` + call site `bindPricing(i)`. **Verified in the browser** via a static harness (DOM shell + `window.VGC_SM` boot + fetch stub, driven by javascript_tool): recipe and bought items both render, live margins recompute on price/cost/VAT/manual-toggle input, net↔gross convert. See memory [[verify-spa-ui-in-browser]] — node --check is not enough for app.js views. |
| 1.20.0 | **Three item prices + margins.** Items gain `price_b2b` (DB **0.19.0**; `price_net` already existed unused). Model: `cost_net` = manufacturing cost (recipe-computed or manual, from 1.19); `price_net` = retail/B2C; `price_b2b` = wholesale. Both prices stored **net**, gross derived from `vat_rate`. shape_item adds `price_net`/`price_gross`/`price_b2b`/`price_b2b_gross` and `retail_margin(_pct)`/`b2b_margin(_pct)` via a `$margin_of` closure: margin = price − cost, pct = margin/price×100 (ex-VAT both sides, % OF price — the app-wide convention). Null-safe when either side is unset. sanitize_item + item_input allow the two prices. UI: form Pricing section gains a Selling-prices block (net+gross pair each) with a **live margin line** (`wirePrice()` + `currentCost()`, which reads the recipe cost or the manual override); item detail hero relabelled "Manufacturing cost" and gains `priceMarginStrip()`. **i18n fix (root cause of the user's confusion):** `Unit cost*` was translated «Единична цена» = "unit *price*"; corrected to «Себестойност» (cost), matching Purchases' «Себест.». **No data migration** (user dismissed the choice): existing `cost_net` left as-is; if those values are really sale prices, a products-only `cost_net`→`price_net` copy is offered but not run. |
| 1.19.0 | **Physical-store allocation + manual cost override.** Items gain `cost_manual`, `store_stocked`, `store_qty` (DB **0.18.0**), plus a new `store_ledger` table. New `class-store.php` (`VGC_SM_Store`): `store_qty` is a cache, `store_ledger` the truth (SUM), same pattern as movements/stock. `distribute($id,$target)` moves units workshop↔store — writes ledger only, **total stock untouched** (guarded: target ≤ stock_qty). `sell($id,$qty)` writes ledger −qty AND a `store_sale` movement −qty, so a store sale is the one bucket move that also reduces stock. `clamp($id)` trims the bucket when it would exceed stock; called from `record_movement()` on any negative delta (soft dependency via `class_exists`), so a workshop ship/scrap auto-trims the store slice. REST: `POST /items/{id}/store-distribute {target}` and `/store-sell {qty}`. shape_item adds `store_stocked`/`store_qty`/`store_workshop`. **Cost:** `cost_computed = has_bom && !cost_manual`, so a recipe item can override its BOM cost by hand; shape adds `cost_manual`/`has_bom`/`cost_calc` (the BOM cost always, for the live form toggle). UI: item form gets a store checkbox + a manual-cost toggle that reveals `#f-cost-manual-block`; item detail gets a Physical store card (at store / at workshop stat tiles + Distribute/Sold-at-store, prompt()-driven like Publish). Verified with a stubbed DB: distribute holds total constant, sale drops both, guards + clamp hold, cache = ledger SUM. **Scope:** one store, allocation-bucket model (not full per-location movements); prices still never pushed to the shop. |
| 1.18.0 | **Partners are bidirectional; `partners.type` is dead.** The declared type gated the whole partner screen (`isOut`/`isIn`) and the Purchases vendor picker, so a shop we supply could not also supply us. Direction belongs to the document, not the partner — all three gates removed, the `pa-type` dropdown and `PARTNER_TYPES` deleted, and `save` no longer sends `type`. The **column and its sanitiser remain** (no migration; existing rows keep `customer`) but nothing reads them — `VGC_SM_Partners::all()`'s `type` filter was already dead code (no caller ever passed it). Replaced by **observed** roles: new `VGC_SM_Partners::roles( $id )` → `{customer, supplier}` via short-circuiting EXISTS queries over orders/purchases with a notes(direction) fallback (2 queries when both hit, 4 worst case); surfaced as `is_customer`/`is_supplier` on `shape_partner`, rendered by `partnerRole()`. `shape_partner[we_owe]` now adds `VGC_SM_Purchases::balance_owed()` — it counted only notes before, so purchase debt was invisible on the partner page. `get_partner` returns `purchases`; the partner screen gained a Purchases card, a New purchase action and `[data-purchase]` click-through. **Cost note:** the partners list now runs roles() + two balance_owed() loops per partner; fine at their scale, worth batching if partner counts grow. |
| 1.17.0 | **Purchases — the inbound mirror of Orders.** New `class-purchases.php` + tables `vgc_sm_purchases` / `_purchase_lines` / `_purchase_payments` (DB **0.17.0**); items gain `price_net`. Same delta-reconcile contract as orders: `applied_in` / `applied_held` are what the ledgers hold, `reconcile()` writes only the difference. **mode `buy`** → `record_movement('receive')`, stock up, owe received−returned. **mode `consignment`** → writes `consignment_ledger` direction **`in`** (the existing held bucket), so `VGC_SM_Notes::held()`, the Held screen and shop publishing pick it up unchanged; owe only `qty_sold`. Flipping mode consignment→buy needs no special action — the next reconcile emits the delta (held→0, stock→received−returned). `billable_qty()` mirrors orders' full/on_sale. **VAT:** `vendor_vat_registered` is snapshotted per document so editing the partner cannot restate a settled purchase; an unregistered vendor's lines are forced to `vat_rate = 0`, which makes the shared `strip_vat()` a no-op — their price is final. **Markup, not margin** (user's explicit choice): `sell_net = net_cost × (1+markup/100)`; `sell_gross` uses the ITEM's vat_rate (our sale VAT), never the vendor's; `margin_from_markup()` exists so the UI shows both (20% markup = 16.7% margin) and the number Orders reports later is never a surprise. Verified: 10.00 final from an unregistered vendor and 12.00 gross from a registered one both give cost 10.00 and shelf 14.40. UI: `viewPurchases`/`viewPurchase`, `.vgc-sm-pline` grid (mirrors `.vgc-sm-oline`), inline New-item panel (no modal system exists). Prices are never pushed to the shop — deliberate; most of this stock sells in the physical location. |
| 1.16.1 | **Stat row fixed — a class-name collision, not a layout tweak.** `.vgc-sm-stat` was already defined (app.css ~line 398) by a replaced component as `display:flex; align-items:flex-end; justify-content:space-between; flex-wrap:wrap`, with `> span` / `strong` children. The 1.15.0 tiles reused the name and never declared `display`, so the older rule won at equal specificity and every tile rendered as a flex row with the figure and caption pushed apart. The old block was **dead** (no markup referenced it) and is deleted. Tiles are now built by `stat(label, value, mod)` / `qual()` / `statRow(tiles)` helpers: caption div first, value div second. `statRow()` filters nulls, computes `--stat-cols = ceil(n/2)` and pads with one blank `.vgc-sm-stat` on an odd count (the divider background showed through the empty cell). CSS: base is `repeat(2, minmax(0,1fr))`; `@media (min-width:900px)` uses `repeat(var(--stat-cols,5), minmax(0,1fr))` — auto-fit cannot know a count that changes with the data, and strung all nine tiles across one line. |
| 1.16.0 | **`prices_include_vat` now governs the whole row, cost included.** It only ever reached `unit_price`, so a gross-typed `unit_cost` was subtracted at full value from a VAT-stripped price — every margin on a gross order was understated by the VAT on cost. New private `VGC_SM_Orders::strip_vat( $amount, $line, $incl )` is the single strip; `net_unit()` and the new public `net_cost()` both delegate to it, so the two can no longer diverge. `totals()` uses `net_cost()`; so does `line_margin` in the REST order payload (class-rest-api.php, which had its own raw copy of the formula). `save_lines()` takes `$incl` and **grosses up the `unit_cost_net()` default** when the order is gross-typed — the BOM computes net, so an untouched default would otherwise be stripped of VAT it never had. UI: `vatBasis()` appends a live «с ДДС»/«без ДДС» marker (`.vgc-sm-oline__basis`) to the Price and Cost headers. **Migration risk:** costs stored before this on `prices_include_vat=1` orders were net (BOM autofill) or gross (hand-typed) — indistinguishable in the data, so no migration was written; hand-typed ones become correct, autofilled ones now under-count. |
| 1.15.1 | **Margin % fix + VAT semantics on the statement.** `statement()[totals][margin_pct]` divided margin (ex-VAT on both sides) by **gross** turnover while `VGC_SM_Orders::totals()` divides by **net** — the same order reported two different margins. `statement()` now accumulates `turnover` (**net**), `vat` and `billed` (**gross**) separately; `margin_pct` = margin / **net** turnover, matching the order screen. `avg_doc` is gross (it pairs with the Total column). Statement tiles: Turnover carries an "excl. VAT" qualifier (`.vgc-sm-stat__q`), plus new Billed (incl. VAT) and VAT (shown only when non-zero) tiles. i18n: added `Quantity`/`Unit price`/`SKU`, which were rendering in English. |
| 1.15.0 | Statement stats: `statement()[totals]` gains `turnover`/`paid`/`margin`/`margin_pct`/`items`/`orders`/`avg_doc` (margin only from orders — notes have no cost snapshot; item counts come from one grouped query over note lines + order billable qty). UI is a **KPI row of stat tiles** (`.vgc-sm-statrow`), not a chart: values wear ink tokens, colour is spent only on state (overdue) or signed direction (profit) and never without its label. Dropped `tabular-nums` from `.vgc-sm-kpi__v` — tabular is for columns, not display-size standalone values. |
| 1.14.0 | `VGC_SM_Notes::statement()` now merges **orders and notes into one timeline** (it only read notes before, so order money was missing entirely). Every statement line carries `total`/`paid`/`balance` + `kind` (note\|order): a note is all-or-nothing (issued = owed in full, settled = paid in full); an order has a real payment trail. Completed orders contribute 0 outstanding, like settled notes. Orders list, partner orders card and the statement all show Total + Paid columns. |
| 1.13.0 | **Partial payments** (`order_payments`, DB 0.16.0) — `add_payment()`/`delete_payment()`/`paid_total()`; `totals()[paid]` is the sum of the trail. **Overdue fix**: `overdue` now also requires `balance > 0.005` (a paid order past its date was wrongly flagging). Orders surfaced on the partner screen (`get_partner` returns `orders`), and `shape_partner[owed]` = notes balance **+** open-order balances. |
| 1.12.0 | Display pieces moved to their own section in the order editor (no price/VAT/margin fields; excluded from totals via `billable_qty()`), and `orders.prices_include_vat` (DB 0.15.0) lets prices be typed VAT-inclusive — `VGC_SM_Orders::net_unit()` derives the net, the typed figure is stored untouched. |
| 1.11.0 | **Orders** — a mutable, order-centric model replacing the note-per-event dance for customer orders. `class-orders.php`, `orders`/`order_lines` (DB 0.14.0). Ordered/sent/sold/returned per line on one screen; payment mode `full`\|`on_sale`; transport + payer; paid/balance; **margin** from a snapshotted `unit_cost`; complete/reopen. Editing reconciles the ledgers by delta (`applied_out`/`applied_pile`). Stock notes remain for ad-hoc/inbound movements and share the same ledgers. |
| **1.10.0** | **Payment terms, refundable returns, display flag, print fixes** (DB 0.13.0). `partners.payment_terms_days` → `stock_notes.due_date` on issue (only for `is_money_note()` types; credits never fall due); statement gains due/overdue + an `overdue` total. `stock_notes.is_refund` on a `return_in`: credits `balance_owed`, skips the consignment write **and** its pre-flight (those goods were billed, not lent). `is_display` on note lines **and** the consignment ledger splits the pile into sellable/display pools — `outstanding()` takes a `$display` filter, sale reports can only draw on the sellable pool, `outstanding_all()` returns `display_qty`. Print: `@page A4 portrait`, cards `break-inside:auto` (they *must* flow), `thead` repeats, rows never split, `.vgc-sm-tablewrap` overflow visible (it was clipping), thumbs hidden. |
| 1.9.0 | **Note dates, "already sent", destinations, per-line VAT + notes, partner VAT** (DB 0.12.0). `note_date` drives `issued_at` on issue; `skip_stock` makes `issue()`/`cancel()` bypass the stock ledger *and* its pre-flight (consignment still applies); `location_id` = destination (validated against the partner's own locations). Lines gain `vat_rate`/`line_vat`/`line_gross`/`line_note`; net↔gross auto-fill is client-side, net is what's stored. **Balances + statements now sum `total_gross`** — one-time `backfill_vat()` sets legacy notes to gross=net/0% so they still read correctly. Partner `vat_registered`/`vat_number`. Editor lines are one flex row (`.vgc-sm-nline`). `list_partners` now returns `locations` via `locations_map()` (one query). |
| 1.8.0 | **Pull filters + virtual default** (needs Bridge 0.4.0): category (`product_cat` slug) and product-type filters on the pull screen, populated from `/shop/product-categories`. Bridge `shape_product` adds `virtual`/`downloadable`; `list_products` takes `category`/`type`. Auto-tick now skips `virtual` products (shown with a tag) as well as `already`. |
| 1.7.1 | **Settings hub**: the four admin config screens (Connection=`/settings`, `/translations`, `/team`, `/audit`) are grouped under one **Settings** sidebar entry with a shared tab bar (`settingsTabs()` + `SETTINGS_TABS`); `activeKey()` maps all four to `#/settings`. Help moved to its own bottom navlist (`NAV_HELP`). UI-only, no schema change. |
| **1.7.0** | **Roles + audit log**: four access levels (viewer/operator/manager/admin) via `VGC_SM_Access::level()`/`at_least()`/`set_level()` (user-meta `vgc_sm_level`); every REST route gated by `$write`/`$mgr`/`$admin`; boot `perms` + level drive UI gating. `class-audit.php` (`VGC_SM_Audit::log/query`) + `audit` table; logged at the mutation handlers and on `wp_login`/`wp_logout`. New admin screens **Team** (`#/team`) and **Activity log** (`#/audit`); DB_VERSION 0.11.0. |

| **1.153.0** | **The company seams.** `VGC_SM_Company` (a table set + an option set per company; company 1's names unchanged), every helper and per-company option key through it, `run_as()`/`each()`, the `plugins_loaded` migration loop per company (`vgc_sm_flag()`), the crons fanned out, deferred init work capturing its company, the two function-local memos made class statics, access meta per company (`vgc_sm_level_c{N}`), the uninstaller's sweep. No user-visible change; the staging snapshot of every table, option and meta key was byte-identical before and after. |
| **1.206.0** | **Partner categories; an invoice number + link on orders and purchases; list search.** Owner: categories "such as suppliers, customers, expenses (rent, water, power, accounting)" and "the number of the invoice and a link for an external source of this invoice"; answers 1a (several per partner, own list on Categories), 2a (tags + filter tabs), 3a (one invoice per document), 4a (document, list row, list search). **DB 0.70.0.**
- **Partner categories:** shared tables `partner_categories` (id, name 120, sort_order) + `partner_category_links` (partner_id, category_id, UNIQUE pair) — SHARED_TABLES, helpers, uninstall, companyseam 53; shared flag `vgc_sm_partner_cats_v1` creates them and `VGC_SM_Partner_Categories::seed()` (Suppliers, Customers, Rent, Water, Power, Accounting — in Bulgarian when the language is bg; only into an empty list). Class: all() with partner counts, add / rename / remove (refusing empty 400 and a taken name in any case 409; remove clears its links), get, for_partners(ids) in list order, set_for_partner (exactly these, unknown and repeated ids dropped). Routes `GET/POST /partner-categories` ($auth/$write), `PUT/DELETE /partner-categories/{id}` ($write), audit partner_category.add/rename/remove. `GET /partners` adds each row's `categories` and the top-level `partner_categories`; `GET /partners/{id}` adds `categories` + `partner_categories`; `category_ids` saved by save_partner_relations. Roles (Customer/Supplier) still observed, unchanged.
- **Screens:** Categories gets a Partner categories section (`categories-partner`, ids `p<id>`, admin.js `catUrl()`), with counts. Partners list: `partners-tabs` (All, a tab per category with its count, No category; `#/partners?cat=`, router now passes the query), row tags (`.vgc-sm-pcat`); form `partner-categories` chips (`.pa-cat`); page header `partner-cats`.
- **Invoices:** orders and purchases `invoice_no VARCHAR(64)`, `invoice_url VARCHAR(500)`. `VGC_SM_Orders::invoice_ref($data)` — the one rule (trim, '' → null, number ≤ 64, link only `^https?://` with no spaces/quotes/angles, esc_url_raw http/https, ≤ 500, else 400 vgc_sm_invoice_no / vgc_sm_invoice_url); Orders::save and Purchases::save apply it only when the keys are sent (an older queued save leaves them). REST order save passes them on has_param; shape_order / shape_purchase return them. The seller's invoice crosses to the buyer's mirror purchase (Intercompany payload + write_mirror); a transferred order takes it along. core `V.invoiceRef(no, url)` (a link only for a web address, target _blank, rel noopener noreferrer) and `V.invoiceUrlOk`; order / purchase details `order-invoice-no/-url`, `purchase-invoice-no/-url`, header `order-invoice` / `purchase-invoice`; list rows show the number; `ol-search` / `pl-search` filter by number, partner or invoice, keeping the focus. i18n 1.206.0 (and the partner row's orders/purchases/owes words); help: Partners → Categories, Orders → the header.
- **Tests:** new `pcats206` (class on SQLite with the installed columns, routes, invoice_ref), `pcats206ui` (jsdom), `pcats206look` (Chrome, Bulgarian); order194ui and purchasediscui pin the invoice on the page and its refusal; intercompany / ordertransfer pin the invoice crossing (their tables gained the columns); payloadkeys accounts for category_ids; corelift + trailstub carry invoiceRef/invoiceUrlOk. 43/43 killed mutants. 198 harnesses, 8622 checks. Staging: schema in every company, the starting list, a throwaway category, partner, quote and purchase through the real routes, a javascript: link refused, all cleaned up. |
| **1.205.0** | **Breadcrumbs on every page; the phone's back wherever there is one; real headings on seven pages.** From the parked survey (2026-10-01: two pages had crumbs, ~18 one back link, purchases none); owner's answers 1a (a fixed trail from the record), 2a (phone back on every page with a trail), 3a (real h1 on product, recipe, location, partner, count, order, purchase). No schema change.
- **core.js:** `crumbs(steps, o)` — one `nav.vgc-sm-back.vgc-sm-crumbs[data-trail]`, steps `{ label, href, key }` top first (empty ones skipped, names escaped, the back arrow on the first, `.vgc-sm-crumbs__sep` between, `data-crumb` keys, o.devid / o.label / o.noprint); `itemTrail(i, self)` = Products|Materials / category [/ product]; exported V.crumbs, V.itemTrail, V.syncBack. `docHeader()`'s number is now an h1. `showsBack()` adds purchase, run fix, location, sale, error, pull; `syncBack()` (called by `screen()` after every non-placeholder draw) shows `#vgc-sm-back` also on any page with a trail (a location's Sales). `V.routes` counts router calls; the back button calls history.back() only when the app has shown more than one page, else goes to the trail's nearest step.

- **One rule:** `VGC_SM_Cost_Split::labour_line($item, $qty, $held, $cost, &$memo)` — our units (qty − held) × labour_per_unit, never more than the cost; labour_of() is now a loop over it.

Sales screen: Your labour → What you earn → Profit as a business. | **1.203.0** | **Merging a duplicate product into another, with a history note and Undo; the shop pull no longer duplicates.** Owner: "merge duplicated products imported from the shop — when I remove a product I can choose to attribute all actions related to this product to another one"; answers 1a (the kept product answers to both shop codes), 2 (a note on the kept product with Undo). **DB 0.69.0.**
- **`VGC_SM_Item_Merge`** (class-item-merge.php): `refs()` FINDS every table from the `vgc_sm_*_table()` helpers that exists and has `item_id` / `component_item_id` / `output_item_id` (SHOW TABLES / SHOW COLUMNS / SHOW INDEX: keys and unique keys; a SHARED_TABLES table with company_id is filtered to this company). `refuse()`: missing, itself, kept archived, different kind or is_service, a recipe loop (either is an ingredient of the other's recipe). `preview()` (moves / dropped per table label, recipe move|drop|none, stock, fills, codes). `merge()` in one transaction: both have a recipe → the removed one's bom + bom_items + bom_extras + its lines' alts deleted (journaled); every row re-pointed unless a unique key says the kept product already has it (then deleted, journaled whole); journal keys are the keys AFTER the change (the column may be part of the key); sale_refunds.detail JSON rewritten; the removed item deleted; kept: empty FILL fields taken (woo_sku/woo_product_id as a pair only when it has no link), SUM fields added (shop_held, store_qty), the removed one's codes (sku, woo_sku, '#'+woo_product_id, its own aliases) added to `items.shop_aliases` (JSON); recompute_stock; shelves rebuilt from the ledger (portable delete+insert); row in per-company `item_merges` (summary for the note, journal LONGTEXT). `undo()`: refused when undone, kept gone (merged away since — undo that first), the id back already, another product holds the SKU; re-inserts the item, re-points back by journal keys (only rows still on the kept product), re-inserts deleted rows, restores refund texts, kept fills only where unchanged since, sums back, codes removed, stocks + shelves rebuilt, marked undone. Audit item.merge / item.merge_undo.
- **Shop:** `VGC_SM_Repository::get_item_by_shop_code($code, $product_id)` — woo_sku, sku, woo_product_id, then shop_aliases (`_matched_by`). `VGC_SM_Shop_Orders::match()` uses it; `/shop/pull` matches existing products with it (it used to look at the plain SKU only — the source of the duplicates) and skips an alias match (the kept product's own link untouched).
- **Routes** ($mgr): `GET/POST /items/{id}/merge` (`into`), `POST /item-merges/{id}/undo`; `GET /items/{id}` adds `merges` and `shop_aliases`. Schema: items.shop_aliases TEXT, table item_merges (helper, create_tables, uninstall; companyseam 51).
- **Screen:** Remove card and the recipe-blocked card offer **Merge into another product** (`#rm-merge`), archived products beside Restore (`data-act=merge`); `mergeCard` / `mergeResults` / `mergePreview` (`item-merge`, `item-merge-preview`), `mergePanel()` in catalogue.js (same kind only, seq-guarded preview, armConfirm, lands on the kept product); `mergesCard` (`item-merges`) with **Undo the merge** (`data-mgundo`, managers). i18n block 1.203.0; help (Removing items).
- **Tests:** new `merge203` (the real class + Repository on SQLite with SHOW TABLES/COLUMNS/INDEX answered from the catalogue; Undo exact table by table), `merge203ui` (jsdom), `merge203look` (Chrome, Bulgarian); shoporders / shoppull176 stand-ins given get_item_by_shop_code; companyseam 51. 25/25 killed mutants. 190 harnesses, 8284 checks. Staging: create-then-clean-up merge + undo through the real routes on MySQL (0.11 s), refs() = the 22 product columns, and the shop pull: a merged duplicate's product pulled again not recreated, a product found by its shop code, a new one created. |
| **1.202.0** | **Translation mode: click any text, change its wording, save.** Owner: "Translations are very hard to apply ... create a translations mode where I will be able to click and edit every string on the site and save the update." DB 0.68.0 (no schema change).
- **core.js** (block before `router()`): `setTranslateMode(on)` / `applyTranslateMode()` — localStorage `vgc_sm_tmode`, `isAdmin()` only, applied at the end of core's load so the app AND the till get it; `V.setTranslateMode`, `V.translateModeOn`. `tmCatalogue()` = `GET /translations` once into `_i18nCat`; `tmIndex()` = shown text (STR or the key) → keys, every key → itself, a length-sorted list for parts, and a regex per string with `%s`/`%1$s` holes. Capture-phase document listeners: `click` (Editing on, not our own UI): `tmTextsAt()` (the text node under the pointer via caretRangeFromPoint/caretPositionFromPoint, the element's own text, its whole text when ≤ 400 chars, a select's options, a button input's value, placeholder/title/aria-label up three levels) → `tmCandidates()` {exact, parts} → `tmOpen()`: one exact → the editor (Back to the list when there were more), none → a new string exactly as shown, otherwise the pick list with "translate exactly as shown"; preventDefault + stopPropagation so the click never acts; `mousedown` on a SELECT blocked; `mouseover` outlines (`.vgc-sm-tmhot`); Escape closes. `tmSave()` posts `{lang, strings:{key: value}}` ('' = built-in), updates STR / HAS_STR / `_i18nCat`, re-indexes and `tmPatch()`es every text node and attribute showing the old words — no redraw. `.vgc-sm-tmbar` (Editing switch, Exit; above the phone nav via --vgc-nav-h), `.vgc-sm-tmpop` (English never through translateTree; buttons via t()).
- **Server:** `VGC_SM_I18n::known_keys()` = catalogue ∪ every override key (added strings); `addable($key)`: non-blank, ≤ 300 bytes, a letter, unchanged by sanitize_text_field (no markup, no line breaks); `save_overrides()` takes known or addable keys; `GET /translations` lists known_keys with `added`.
- **Screens:** Settings card `settings-tmode` (#s-tmode); Translations screen **Edit on the page** (#tr-onpage). i18n block 1.202.0; help (Language topic).
- **Tests:** new `tmode202` (the real VGC_SM_I18n), `tmode202look` (Chrome, Bulgarian, real clicks at text points, desk + phone + a manager below admin). 17/17 killed mutants. 187 harnesses, 8189 checks. Staging: create-then-clean-up through the real routes (a catalogue string edited and put back, a new text added and removed, junk refused, the option as it began). |
| **1.201.0** | **The time zone, chosen in Settings; the stored times converted, with an exact Undo.** Owner's item 3 ("time stamps are not in my local time ... convert the existing time stamps"), answers 4 (configurable in Settings) and 5b (convert + Undo). Every time the app writes is `current_time('mysql')` = WordPress's timezone, which was UTC (gmt_offset 0, no timezone_string). **DB 0.68.0.**
- **`VGC_SM_Timezone`** (class-timezone.php): `zone()` (wp_timezone_string, '+00:00' read as UTC), `zones()`, `state()`, `record()`, `can_undo()`, `shift($v, $from, $to, $keep_day)` — the one rule: each value at its own date (DST as it was); times 00:00:00 / 12:00:00 / 23:59:59 are a picked DAY and never move (a backdated movement is the day at 23:59:59); `$keep_day` keeps a stock note's `note_date` on `issued_at` (note_date + the clock of the moment) and moves only the clock; NULL / '' / zero dates / rubbish untouched. `tables()` FINDS what to convert: `SHOW TABLES LIKE {prefix}vgc_sm_%` (every company's set), `SHOW COLUMNS` → datetime/timestamp columns and the PRI key; skips the journal. `plan($to)` counts and gives examples (last sale / push / movement), changing nothing. `apply($to, $convert)`: refusals (bad zone, same zone, busy lock option `vgc_sm_tz_busy`), one transaction: journal cleared, every table read by key in batches of 400, journal rows inserted BEFORE the batched `UPDATE … SET col = CASE WHEN <key> THEN … END` (composite keys matched on every column); ROLLBACK on any error; then `timezone_string` = $to; record option `vgc_sm_tz_conversion` {from, to, at, by, rows, tables, old {timezone_string, gmt_offset}, undone}; a change WITHOUT converting marks an open record `superseded` (no Undo). `undo()`: every row of every table — journaled and unchanged since → the old value exactly; anything else (written or rewritten since) shifted new→old zone; journal cleared; the old options restored; record undone. Audit `timezone_change` / `timezone_undo`.
- **Schema:** shared table `tz_journal` (id, tbl, pk, col, old_value/new_value VARCHAR(19)) — helper `vgc_sm_tz_journal_table()`, in SHARED_TABLES, create_tables, flag `vgc_sm_tz_journal_v1`, uninstall; companyseam now 50 tables. Routes (admin): `GET/POST /settings/timezone`, `GET /settings/timezone/plan?zone=`, `POST /settings/timezone/undo`. Boot (app and till): `tz`, `today`. Audit prune and the shop's chase window now cut with `wp_date()` (site clock).
- **Screen:** Settings `tzCard`/`tzBody` (`settings-timezone`, `-now`, `-plan`, `-done`), `bindTimezone()` at module scope in admin.js (plan per choice with a seq guard, Save/Undo via armConfirm, `boot.tz`/`boot.today` follow). **`V.today()`** in core.js: the date in `boot.tz` via Intl (formatToParts), falling back to the device's local day — replaces every `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` (consignment, orders, purchases, print letterhead). i18n block 1.201.0; help topic `timezone`.
- **Tests:** new `tz201` (the real class on SQLite; its own wpdb subclass answers SHOW TABLES / SHOW COLUMNS from sqlite_master / PRAGMA; a trigger forces a mid-way failure to prove the rollback), `tz201ui` (jsdom), `tz201look` (Chrome, Bulgarian); companyseam 50. 25/25 killed mutants. 185 harnesses, 8132 checks. Staging: the journal created; tables() equal to information_schema's DATETIME columns and keys; the plan on real data; the conversion's SQL run on TEMPORARY copies (converted, put back exactly); one journal row written and deleted; real data and the zone untouched (the real round trip waits for the owner's word). |

Routes `GET /locations/{id}/log` ($auth) and `GET /locations/{id}/flow` ($mgr, `flow` cast to an object).

| **1.197.0** | **Till gifts: not lines, many at once, given uncounted and settled at the closing count.** Owner's answers: only FREE extras leave the lines (1a); still tracked, said on the sale's page (2a); −/+ and a tap on the name adds 1 (3); given beyond stock and settled at the end, never on To make and send (4 ii). Decided: the stock rule covers every quick extra (charged too); an event cannot close while an extra is below zero. DB 0.67.0 (no schema change).
- **Server:** checkout records no backlog for an `extra` line (`$short > 0.0001 && empty( $p['extra'] )`): the location simply goes below zero. `VGC_SM_Events::settlement()` rows carry `is_extra` / `extra_price` (from `extra_prices()`); an extra is ready only at exactly 0 (below zero is a line to count). `settle()`: an extra counted beyond the books is not refused — the difference is `push()`ed from the workshop (allow_short, note 'Taken to %s uncounted, booked at the closing count') and returned as `sent`; short of the books becomes a checkout line `extra => true` at the extra's price (0 for a gift). Ordinary lines counted beyond the books are still refused (`vgc_sm_settle_over`). `outstanding()` adds `given` (SUM(−qty) of extras below zero); `close()` refuses on it with its own message.
- **Till:** `state.gifts {item_id: qty}` beside `state.cart`, cleared with it (location switch, dock clear, after checkout); `addExtra()` of a free extra → `stepGift(id, +1)`; checkout appends the gifts as `extra: true, unit_price 0` lines. extrasRow: a free extra is `.vgc-sm-posgift` (`data-gift-add` name button, `data-gift-step` −/+ 40px targets, `data-gift-n` count); a charged one keeps `data-extra` with its price; the extra line's tag is 'Extra'.
- **Sale's page:** `isGift(l)` / `givenLine(lines)` in locations.tpl.js: gifts filtered out of the lines, `receipt-given` under them. **Closing count screen:** an extra's counted box starts at max(0, books), never `is-bad`, no price box; the leftover cell says N booked as sent / N given away (or its money); Settle and Settle all skip the over-count refusal for extras; the close card names extras below zero.
- **Tests:** new `gifts197` (real checkout + closing count on SQLite, from showpiece181's setup by make_gifts197.py), `gifts197ui` (jsdom); `tillextras` (Chrome) rewritten for gifts (count, −/+, name tap, next sale starts at 0); `extrasdata` (nothing on the list); `primary` given the extras table. 24/24 killed mutants. 179 harnesses, 7948 checks. Staging: create-then-clean-up event + item on MySQL, three gifts with nothing sent, settled, closed. |
| **1.196.0** | **Payments on stock notes; every refund on its own day; the issued note's page fixed.** Owner's answers: part payments on notes (1b), notes marked paid before this left undated (2a), refunds still off the sale's month in P&L — only the cash view reads the refund's day (3a). DB 0.67.0.
- **Note payments:** per-company `note_payments` (note_id, paid_on DATE, amount, note, user_id, created_at); columns in `VGC_SM_Install::note_payments_columns()`, dbDelta + `ensure_note_payments_table()` behind flag `note_payments_v1`. `VGC_SM_Notes`: `takes_payments()` (= `is_money_note`, never a refund return), `payments()`, `paid_map($ids)` (one query), `money_state($note, $paid, $count)` → paid / balance / paid_state unpaid|part|paid / paid_undated (settled with no rows = paid in full, day not kept), `add_payment()` (refusals before writing: no money, not issued/already paid, < 0.01, > what is left, bad date, future, before issued_at; settles at full), `delete_payment()` (a settled note left short is issued again), `mark_paid($id, $paid_on)` = what is left as one payment (a no-money note just settles), `unsettle()` deletes every payment, `cancel()` refused while payments exist (also stops a till refund cancelling a maker's part-paid note — as a settled one always did). `get()` carries payments. Balances (`left_on()`), the statement (paid/balance/overdue per note) read what is left.
- **REST:** `shape_note` adds takes_payments, paid, balance, paid_state, paid_undated, payments[] (when loaded); `with_paid($rows)` for lists (notes list, partner page). `POST /notes/{id}/payments` ($write), `DELETE /notes/{id}/payments/{pid}` ($mgr), `POST /notes/{id}/paid` reads `paid_on`.
- **Refund history:** per-company `sale_refunds` (sale_id, kind goods|extra, refunded_at, net, vat, gross, detail TEXT JSON [{line_id,item_id,name,qty,gross}], note, backfilled, user_id) — NOT `lines` (reserved in MySQL 8). `refund()` writes one row per call (same `$now` as the sale's refunded_at); the shop sync writes kind extra for new money beyond the goods (`split_extra()` by the sale's net/gross). `refunds_of()` on the sale shape; `delete_sale()` deletes them; `backfill_refund_history()` once (flag `sale_refund_history_v1`, after `sale_refunds_v1`): one goods row per refunded sale dated refunded_at ?: created_at with detail from the lines, one extra row for ext_refunded, idempotent.
- **Money:** cash out_refunds = `sale_refunds` by DATE(refunded_at); notes in/out = note payments by paid_on + settled notes with no payments by issued_at; ageing notes by what is left (`notes_left()`). P&L unchanged.

| **1.195.0** | **The Money tab (Statistics, manager only).** Owner's answers: a partner order counts when SENT, income and cost together (on_sale by its sale reports' dates); old stock-note sales when issued; income = till + shop + orders + notes, own companies (`partners.company_id > 0`) their own line; costs = goods (snapshots) + business costs minus in-product-cost categories + event costs + transport we pay + lost stock (hand / count scrap, location shrink + gift; never defective or spoiled-in-run, already in the good units' cost); ageing by days late with a no-due-date column; cash from recorded payments, expenses paid on `spent_on`. No schema change.
- **Server:** new `includes/class-money.php` `VGC_SM_Money::report($range)` → `months[]` / `totals` / `previous` (P&L), `cash`, `vat` (null unless registered), `ageing` (as of today), `vendors`. Every period figure grouped by day in SQL (`DATE()`, no `DATE_FORMAT`) and folded into `Y-m` months in PHP by `adder()`, which drops any day outside the period. Orders and purchases summed only through their own `totals()` / `line_shares()` (no SQL copy). New `VGC_SM_Orders::get_many($ids)` (4 queries) and `VGC_SM_Purchases::get_many($ids)` (3), identical to `get()`. `GET /reports/money` (`require_manager`) in `trait-reports.php` via `stats_envelope()`.
- **Rules:** orders `status IN (open, completed)`, dated `date_sent ?: date_ordered ?: created_at` (the statement's), returns in that month; on_sale per item: line net per unit × reported qty by `sold_on`, the unreported rest on the order day, over-reporting scaled down. Till refunds and the shop's `ext_refunded` (ex-VAT by the sale's own net/gross) off the sale's month. Note goods and lost stock at today's cost (`unit_cost_num`, cached per item). Business costs = Overheads' `overhead_net` (purchase-synced rows included, templates out). Cash: refunds by `COALESCE(refunded_at, created_at)` (only the last is kept), order payments by `paid_on` (not quote/requested/transferred), settled notes by `issued_at`, expenses gross minus `purchase_id` rows, purchase payments, event costs; running total from 0. VAT: charged on sales / orders (same split) / sale notes; reclaimable on purchases (`date_received ?: date_ordered ?: created_at`, requests requested/declined/failed out) + expenses gross−net minus `purchase_id` rows + event costs; inbound notes out. Ageing: open orders and purchases with balance > 0.005, issued money notes, an unsettled refund note a not-due credit, accrual bills always no-due-date; `bucket()` midday-to-midday, due today = not due.
- **Screen:** `STATS_TABS` money (mgr) after Sales; `stats.js` `viewMoneyStats` (sort states moneyOwedToYou / moneyYouOwe / moneyVendors, CSVs profit-and-loss (with VAT rows when registered), cash, owed-and-owing, spend-by-supplier); `stats.tpl.js` `moneyPage`, `mtable` (lines × months, Total column only for 2+ months, nought lines hidden unless `keep`, sticky row headers), cards stats-money-tiles/chart/pnl/cashchart/cash/vat/ageing (owed / owing)/vendors. Core `V.pairChart(rows, opts)` (two bars per month on ONE axis, legend, tooltip) and `V.monthName('Y-m')`. Chart tokens `--vgc-chart-a/b` (#EB6834 / #2A78D6, dark #D95926 / #3987E5 — dataviz validator passes; NOT the brand hex, which pwa_php_test reserves for the accent tokens). Bulgarian months Jan–Dec added (the Sales chart's axis was English).

DB 0.66.0. Money statistics moved to 1.195.0.
- **Ordered basis:** `VGC_SM_Orders::line_qty($line, $mode, $basis)` — 'ordered' = max(qty_ordered, billable), display 0 — and `line_shares($order, $basis)` runs the one algorithm on it; `totals()` adds `value_net/vat/gross` (transport they pay included). Balance, owed, overdue, statement, turnover and the mirror read the billed figures only. JS `orderShares(lines, doc, basis)` mirrors it (order194math: 2,000 orders, both bases).
- **Price:** `default_price($partner_id, $item, $incl)` = agreed `Partners::price_for`, else the item's `price_b2b` (grossed at the sale rate when the order is gross-typed), else null; `save_lines` uses it (an unpriced line no longer saves at 0); `GET /orders/default-price` for the screen, which fills a line's price when a product is picked and again (if it filled it) when the partner or the VAT basis changes.

Quotes left out of count_for_partner, turnover_for_partner, the statement, Item_Totals, run-fix, remirror_all; never mirrored while a quote (`on_order_saved`); the mirror's status maps to open/completed; `Order_Transfer::untouched` allows a quote, which arrives as a quote. `shape_order`: `quote`, `sent_state` none|part|all (Σ sent vs Σ max(ordered, sent)), `paid_state` unpaid|part|paid (paid ahead = paid), `can_complete` (open, all sent, nothing owed, nothing unsold at theirs on pay as sold), per line `value_qty`/`value_net`.
- **Screen:** Orders list tabs (`orders-tabs`, `.vgc-sm-tabs--in`, remembered in localStorage `vgc_sm_orders_tab`, default Open, picked tab kept in view), New quote, stage tag + `stateLine()` words, Total = value when nothing billed ("ordered"). Order page: stage badge (`statusBadge` knows quote/order), `order-stage` tags, `order-quote` card with Confirm, `order-complete-offer`, Amount column (plain 2-dp figure, grey italic + "ordered" while on the ordered basis; grid gap 6px to keep its desk width), quote tiles (Quote total), Order value tile while unsent, Sent/Sold/Ret. locked on a quote with the reason, no payments/sales on a quote. Print: title QUOTE/ORDER, status in words, a quote or an unsent order on the ordered basis, a quote without Paid/Balance and "Prepared for"; print labels given Bulgarian.

| **1.192.0** | **The error log (error reports, stage 2).** The 1.167.0 plan's second half: every bug kept on the server so nothing is lost. DB 0.64.0.
- **Data:** shared table `error_log` (in `SHARED_TABLES`, created once under option `vgc_sm_error_log_v1`): code, fingerprint UNIQUE(191), kind js|php|fatal, message, place (file:line), hits, first/last seen and version, company_id, user_id, route, report TEXT (latest), status open|resolved, resolved_in, came_back. Uninstall drops it; reset does not (it is not a company's).
- **Model** (`class-errors.php`): `record()` upserts by fingerprint — the same bug within 60 s updates the report but not `hits`; a resolved bug met on a version `>=` resolved_in reopens with came_back (an older version, a stale phone, stays fixed); another kind's report of the same row (the browser's of a server failure) keeps the row's message and place; more than 30 NEW bugs a minute are held back; fields capped (message 300, place/route 191, report 8000) and stripped of tags. `resolve($id, $open)`, `get`, `query` (status/search/pages/counts, no report in the list), `prune()` (resolved > 90 days, then cap 500 dropping resolved first) once a day. `as_response()` records a route's Throwable (fingerprint `php|Type|where`, the key its ref is made from); `on_shutdown()` (register_shutdown_function) records E_ERROR/PARSE/CORE/COMPILE/USER_ERROR in our own files as `fatal|where`; `server_report()` writes the browser's layout; `scrub()` takes the plugin and WordPress paths out. `code()` hashes UTF-16 units so a Cyrillic key agrees with the browser's `errorCode`. Date math parses and prints in one zone (date(strtotime(...))).
- **Routes** (`rest/trait-errors.php`): POST `/errors` (`$auth`, the till's operator too; the code recomputed here), GET `/errors`, GET `/errors/{id}`, POST `/errors/{id}/resolve` {open} (admin; audit error.resolve / error.reopen).
- **Browser** (core.js): `reportError` also `shipError()`s — straight fetch (no breadcrumb), once a minute per fingerprint, a server failure keyed `php|type|where` to land on the server's row; offline or a 5xx/no answer queues in localStorage `vgc_sm_err_queue` (20 max) flushed on `online` and 4 s after load; a refusal is dropped. Settings → **Errors** tab (admin): `#/errors` (Open/Fixed/All, search, sortable page, came back in red) and `#/error/{id}` (facts, the kept report copyable via `V.rememberReport` + `[data-errcopy]`, Mark fixed / Open again).
- **Tests:** new `errorlog192` (42, SQLite on the real model and routes), `errorlog192ui` (26, incl. the JS/PHP code cross-check with Cyrillic), `errorlog192look` (31, Chrome desk/narrow/phone); companyseam 47 tables. 31/31 mutants. 160 harnesses, 7204 checks. Staging probe 14/14 on MySQL.
- Numbering: the Statistics follow-ups are 1.193.0 (Production and Stock deepened) / 1.194.0 (Money). |
| **1.191.0** | **Discounts on partner orders.** Owner: "when a partner orders products we might agree on partial discounts for some of the products sold" — "3 of 6 at 10%, a clear indication that only 3 are discounted, not an average". Asked: limit = larger of ordered and sent; an amount is off EACH unit; a Sold box chooses which billed units were the discounted ones; also a whole-order discount; the print shows list, discount, total. DB 0.63.0.
- **Data:** per-company `order_line_discounts` (order_id, order_line_id, qty, qty_sold NULL = automatic, discount_type pct|val, discount_value, sort_order) — a layer on the bill, stock and pile never read it; `orders.discount_type/discount_value`. `ensure_order_discount_schema()` behind flag `order_discount_v1`; reset and uninstall know the table.
- **One definition:** `VGC_SM_Orders::line_shares()` (keyed by position): Sold figures first, then blank rows fill what is still billed (never beyond `billable_qty`), money off per row (`unit_discount_off`: pct of each unit, val per unit capped at the price), the whole-order discount off what is left spread by value with the remainder to the last line with value, in the order's own basis, then VAT out; a line with no discount keeps the old formula byte for byte. `totals()` adds subtotal/subtotal_net/discount_line/discount_order/discount_total/discount_net; margin falls by the net discount. `orderShares()` in orders.js mirrors it line for line with a PHP-compatible round4.
- **Saving:** refused before any write when a line's agreed total exceeds max(qty_ordered, qty_sent) (`vgc_sm_discount_over`, naming the product); a Sold figure is clamped to its row; empty rows dropped; display pieces keep none; an absent `discounts` key / `discount_type` param leaves them as they are (older clients, write_request, transfer); reconcile sweeps orphans; delete removes all. REST `shape_order` gives per line discounts[] {applied_qty, amount}, line_list_net, line_net/line_margin after, agreed/applied/marked/oversold/cap.
- **Screen:** + Discount under a priced line (`data-dadd`), rows in `.vgc-sm-odisc` (the line's last grid child): units of the limit, `V.discountControl`, "each" beside an amount, the Sold box, the amount, ×, and the line's words; live in place; the client refuses what the server would; whole-order select + value in the details card; a Discounts tile; Disp. clears a line's discounts; the Record-sale suggestion after the line's discounts; print sub-rows (net unit, gross total) and a Subtotal → Discounts on products → Discount on the order → Net cascade. Statement: Discounts given (sum of discount_net).
- **Companies:** the mirror folds each line's discounts and its share of the order's into one purchase-line amount off, grossed for a buyer who cannot reclaim and scaled for the purchase's billable/received pro-rating (the purchase gets no discount of its own) — the two documents agree to the cent; a moved order carries its rows and the order discount, per-unit amounts and the order amount grossed across a VAT boundary.
- **Tests:** new `orderdisc191` (55, SQLite on the real model, incl. 200 no-discount orders identical to 1.190.0), `orderdisc191stmt` (4, the real statement), `orderdisc191math` (2,000 random orders, JS = PHP to the digit), `orderdisc191ui` (41), `orderdisc191look` (25, Chrome desk/narrow/phone + the Bulgarian print); intercompany +8, ordertransfer +5; companyseam (46 tables, 42 flags) and test-sale-reports updated. 39/39 mutants (the sweep now re-syncs the mirrored sources). 157 harnesses, 7105 checks. Staging probe 14/14 through the routes on MySQL.

| **1.187.0** | **Changing how many are planned.** Owner: "if I add 6 and decide to take 4 I need to delete this entry and add it again." No schema change.
- **Server:** `VGC_SM_Backlog::replan($loc, $item, $qty)` (POST `/locations/{id}/backlog/plan/qty` {item_id, qty}, `$write` like plan) sets the item's PLANNED total (open rows with `sale_id IS NULL`); owed rows are never touched. Fewer comes off the newest plan first (a row used up is `cancelled` with resolved_at, a row cut is decremented in place); more is added to the newest open plan. Same total ⇒ no-op; `< 0.0001` ⇒ `vgc_sm_plan_qty` (Remove is the way off); no plan ⇒ `vgc_sm_plan_none`. No ledger, no movement. Audit `backlog.replan` (was → now).
- **Screen:** `backlogRow` carries `data-backlogrow`; a planned line has a neutral pencil (`data-replan`, `data-planned` = `V.numVal(planned)`, `.vgc-sm-x--edit`) beside Remove, which swaps for `replanEditor` (the planned number prefilled and focused, unit, save ✓ / cancel ✕, and ‘plus N sold, not logged’ on a part-owed line). Enter saves, Escape cancels, a comma is read as a decimal point, nought is refused client-side. The page redraws in place (1.185.0).
- **Tests:** new `replan187` (19, SQLite on the real Backlog and Sales), `replan187ui` (15), `replan187look` (16, Chrome desk/narrow/phone); primaryui stand-ins gained `numVal`. 10/10 mutants. 147/147 harnesses, 6741 checks. Staging probe 11/11 through the route.

Same look as the 1.180.0 makers'-goods card.
- **Sales screen:** the no-price warning (`sales-gaps`, its bold red line is the head), Sold by category (`sales-categories`, ‘N categories’) and Receipts (`sales-receipts` on `sales-list`, ‘N receipts’ from totals; Select and CSV inside the body). Filter, totals and chart are not lists and do not fold. `viewSales` calls `V.bindFolds(APP)` after `screen()`; the receipts search redraws only `#sa-rows`, inside the body.

| **1.185.0** | **The location page drawn again without moving.** Owner: "everytime i use the settle button the view reloads and snaps me to the top." Client only.
- **Cause:** `viewLocation()` painted the loading card first, then `screen()` put back `V.keepY`, but the shelf (`renderInv`) is filled after `screen()`, so on a long page the browser clamped the scroll short. Reproduced in Chrome: the settled line moved 700–1,800 px and the loading card flashed every time.
- **Fix (locations.js):** `viewLocation` paints `loading()` only on arrival (`V.painted !==` the address); a redraw of the same page keeps it until the new one is ready. After the render a microtask (before the browser paints) scrolls so a held element is exactly where it was on the screen (`holdView(id, sel)` → `holdAt[id]` {sel, top}), or back to `V.keepY` when nothing is held. Held: the line after its own Settle and after an undo from Correct (`[data-settlerow="X"]`), the end of the count after Settle all (`[data-settlefoot]`, on both the actions and the ‘every line is settled’ note). `holdAt` is dropped on arrival.
- **Tests:** new `keepview185` (17, Chrome, desk + phone: a long page whose shelf shrinks above the count; the line within 2 px after Settle and after an undo, on every painted frame; the end of the count after Settle all; no loading card). 6/6 mutants (incl. a timer-late restore). 142/142 harnesses, 6649 checks. Staging: installed files carry it.
- Numbering: error-report stage 2 is now 1.186.0; Statistics follow-ups are 1.187.0 / 1.188.0. |
| **1.184.0** | **Correcting a closing-count line.** Owner: a line took 5, rang up 1 and read "nothing left" — "How is this possible", and wants to correct it. No schema change.
- **Story:** `settlement()` rows add `home` (pull backs), `missing` (shrink), `gifted` (gift), `found`, from the location ledger. `took` and `home` are reversal-aware: a transfer row with `reverses` set counts against what it cancels, so an undone pull is not more taken. Shown under the name (`settleStory`, `data-story`).
- **History + undo:** `VGC_SM_Events::line_history($loc,$item)` (GET `/locations/{id}/event/line/{item}`, manager) lists entries oldest first as sent / sold / returned / home / missing / gifted / found / undone, with `can_undo` (open event, not reversed, not a reversal, and a pull back naming its movement, or shrink/gift/found) and a sale's `sale_id`/`sale_number`. `undo_line_entry()` (POST `…/undo` {ledger_id}): the entry must be on this event; a pull back goes through `VGC_SM_Corrections::undo_movement()` (the push-back refuses if the workshop lacks the units); shrink/gift/found through new `VGC_SM_Locations::reverse_entry()` — the opposite row of the same type (showpiece_qty negated, ref_type `correction`), `reverses` linked, original `reversed_at`; undoing a find refuses if its units are gone. Audit `event.correct`. The new ledger row moves the line's watermark, so a settled line reopens.
- **Screen:** **Correct** (`data-settlefix`) on ready and settled lines opens `tr.vgc-sm-fixrow` under the line (sticky + `100cqw` like the piece's answers) listing entries (`data-fixentry`) with Undo (`data-fixundo`, `V.armConfirm`) or the receipt link (`#/sale/ID`); after an undo the page redraws with the other lines' typed figures (`settleKeep`, minus the corrected line, which starts from its units).
- **Tests:** new `correct184` (28, SQLite on the real Events/Locations/Corrections), `correct184ui` (18); `loc181look` 55 in Chrome (story, the panel inside the visible width, Undo in reach); showpiece181/settle182 schemas gained `reverses`/`reversed_at`. 16/16 mutants. 141/141 harnesses, 6632 checks. Staging probe 13/13.
- Numbering: error-report stage 2 is now 1.185.0; Statistics follow-ups are 1.186.0 / 1.187.0. |
| **1.183.0** | **Nothing settled for the owner.** Owner: "remove the fully automatic settle. Mark items only if they should be settled, but don't settle them automatically." No schema change.
- **Server:** `settle()` records the lines it settled in option `count_settled_{loc}` (item_id ⇒ the newest location-ledger row id of that line after the settle; `mark_settled()`). `settlement()` rows: `settled` = nothing left AND a mark exists AND the line's newest ledger row is still that id (`newest_rows()`), so any later push, sale or refund reopens it; `ready` = nothing left AND not settled. The 1.182.0 derived rule (nothing left ⇒ settled) is gone.
- **Screen:** `T.settleDone(r)` = `!!r.settled` (never worked out client-side); `T.settleReady(r)`. A ready row is `tr.is-ready` (info-bg, sticky cell too) with one `colspan=4` cell *Nothing left here — ready to settle* (`data-ready`, text font) and its **Settle**. Ready rows go with Settle all / Save progress as zeros. A line settled with nothing to move toasts *settled, with nothing to count*.
- **Tests:** `settle182` 23 (ready vs settled, owner's settle, reopen, sold out again ⇒ ready not settled), `settle182ui` 28, `loc181look` 49 (ready tint + Settle), `loc181ui` updated. 27/27 mutants (11 new + 16 of 1.182.0 re-run). 139/139 harnesses, 6580 checks. Staging probe 13/13.
- Numbering: error-report stage 2 is now 1.184.0; Statistics follow-ups are 1.185.0 / 1.186.0. |
| **1.182.0** | **The closing count, a line at a time.** No schema change.
- **Server:** `VGC_SM_Events::settlement()` rows carry `settled` = here ≤ 0 and no presentation piece left (counted and settled, or sold out at the till). `settle()` of any subset is how a line settles on its own (it always accepted one); after commit it now calls `drop_from_draft($loc, item_ids)` instead of `clear_draft()`: only the settled lines leave the parked count, and the option is deleted once no row is left.
- **Screen:** `settleRow` has a 9th column `.vgc-sm-settle__act` with **Settle** (`data-settleone`), sticky right with the row's opaque tint; a settled row (`T.settleDone(r)`) is `tr.is-settled` (ok-bg), its boxes replaced by — and *Line settled* (`data-settled`). `settleCounts()` skips settled rows, so **Settle all** (`#ev-settle`, renamed from Settle the count) and Save progress send open lines only; with none open the card says *Every line is settled.* (`data-settleall-done`). A line's Settle runs the same refusals (over-count, unanswered piece, no price), posts that line alone, then stores the other lines' on-screen figures in `settleKeep[locId]` ({counts, dirty}) and redraws; `viewLocation` merges them over the parked count (detailPage `settleCounts` → settleCard `counts`), keeps *Not saved* if they were dirty, and drops `settleKeep` on arrival. The piece's answers are capped to the sheet's visible width via `container-type: inline-size` on the settle table's wrapper and `100cqw`.
- **Tests:** new `settle182` (17, SQLite on the real Events), `settle182ui` (23); `loc181look` +3 in Chrome (a line's Settle in view while the sheet scrolls sideways, the settled tint, Settle all). primaryui/loc181ui updated. 19/19 mutants. 139/139 harnesses, 6566 checks. Staging probe 12/12 through the REST routes.

| **1.181.0** | **The presentation piece.** One more unit sent to a sale location for the table, sold last and only when told, answered for on its return. DB 0.61.0 → 0.62.0.
- **Data:** `location_ledger.showpiece_qty` and `sale_lines.showpiece_qty`, DECIMAL(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, signed like `qty`: how much of that row or line was the piece. dbDelta adds them; `VGC_SM_Install::ensure_showpiece_columns()` (flag `showpiece_v1`) is the safety net. Written only when non-zero.
- **Pool:** `VGC_SM_Locations::showpieces($loc)` (item_id ⇒ units) and `showpiece_at($loc, $item)` = SUM(showpiece_qty), floored at 0. Part of on_hand; `inventory()` rows carry `showpiece`, the till catalogue `at_showpiece`, the location shape `can_showpiece` (= !sells_from_workshop).
- **Push:** `push(..., $allow_short, $showpiece)` adds one unit as one transfer_in row (qty + 1, showpiece_qty 1). Refused at the counter/online shop (`vgc_sm_showpiece_here`) and when one is already there (`vgc_sm_showpiece_there`). Counts against the workshop. `push_many` lines take `showpiece`. REST `/locations/{id}/push` takes `showpiece`.
- **Checkout:** order is ours (on_hand − piece) → a maker's held → the piece only if the line says `showpiece: true`, else `vgc_sm_showpiece` (data item_id, showpiece, regular, held). A declared backlog leaves the piece alone (`uncovered_here(..., $keep)`). `showpiece_only` lines (opts `showpiece_only`, never from REST) take the piece itself. The deduct row carries −piece; the line's `showpiece_qty`. Refund returns the piece first, as the piece only if the table has room (max 1).
- **Home:** `pull(..., $showpiece)` refuses ordinary units beyond on_hand − piece. `settle_showpiece($loc, $item, $qty, home|sold|missing)` — REST pull with `showpiece`: home = flagged pull; sold = a sale of the piece at its price (note "recorded on its return"); missing = flagged shrink at cost. Events: settlement rows `showpiece` / `showpiece_sold`; draft stores the answer; settle refuses `vgc_sm_settle_showpiece` until answered, counts ordinary units against here − piece.
- **Elsewhere:** Corrections undo carries the piece (read off the transfer's ledger row). A location count below the piece takes it (`sp_part`).
- **UI:** push staged `data-stsp` tick; shelf `spQty` n+1; Pull back `data-pullsp` (own row at a desk, `.vgc-sm-pullrow`; own line on a phone, `.vgc-sm-invrow`); closing count `settleSpRow` (`data-sprow`, `data-spans`, sticky on a phone); till tile `spTag`, cart `showpieceRow` (`data-sp`), `spPart()`/`anyUnticked()`, Charge label `blockedBy`; receipt tag `data-linesp`.
- **Tests:** new `showpiece181` (89, SQLite on the real Locations/Sales/Events), `loc181ui` (41), `tillsp181` (29, Chrome), `loc181look` (37, Chrome); `counts` +8 (undo, count). primary/primaryui/heldtill/consignsale/extrasdata/shoppull176 updated for the column. 40/40 mutants. 137/137 harnesses, 6516 checks. Staging probe 21/21 (create, flow, clean up).

| **1.178.0** | **Cost of goods split by the recipes.** New `VGC_SM_Cost_Split`:
- `labour_per_unit($item_id, &$memo)` sums `bom_extras` of kind 'labour' (per item, or per run ÷ yield, as `recipe_roll`) plus each made component's labour × `demand_per_item`, recursively. A subcontracted service (`supplier_of`>0) is 0 and not walked; no recipe → 0. Loop guard MAX_DEPTH 32; the memo is passed in, never static.
- `labour_of(lines)`: our units only (sold − held), `min(own × per, line cost)`, a zero-cost line none.
- `Sales::labour_in_cogs()` groups by item and cost over the same WHERE; a NULL cost counts as today's `unit_cost_of`, as `cogs_by_sale`.

Both show `is-part` lines under What the goods cost. locations.js fetches the P&L for every location (event costs only for events).
- Today's recipes applied to all sales; the frozen line cost is not split.

| **1.176.0** | **Fix: a shop order re-made on every pull.** The pull re-reads the last-modified order every run (inclusive cursor), relying on `apply()` being idempotent. `differs()` compared the stored sale with the shop's RAW figures, including the order total, so anything the import could not reproduce made a just-imported sale 'differ' again. Examples: a total a stotinka off its lines when WooCommerce rounds tax on the total; a name checkout's `sanitize_text_field` changes; an empty fee name that checkout drops; a product unit rounded to 4 places. Each pull then ran delete_sale + import, walking the goods out and back (online: `to_location` − / `from_location` +, stock netting out), and gave the sale a new id. Owner saw W-5137 'Sold on receipt' in a product's history on every pull. Now `differs()` compares with `expected_lines()`: lines_for normalised exactly as checkout stores them (product unit 4dp, others 6dp, line = qty×unit to 4dp, label sanitised, cut to 200, sku to 64, dropped when empty). The order total is no longer compared, and the message total is what the shop's lines come to. `lines_for` names a nameless shipping/fee line 'Shipping'/'Fee' (kept with its money). Checkout cuts an external line's name/sku to its columns. Safety net: after a correction the new sale is re-checked. If it still differs, option `shop_orders_stuck[ext_id] = why` (≤100) + audit `shop.correct_stuck`, and later pulls skip correcting it for the same why. Harness `shoppull176` (29, real Sales/Locations/Shop_Orders on SQLite: W-5137, a real change once, 8 awkward orders stable on the 2nd/3rd pull, a trigger-made unfixable mismatch stuck once then left); 10/10 mutants incl. the old comparison; staging create-then-clean-up probe through the real apply() (12). Numbering: error-report stage 2 is now 1.177.0, Statistics follow-ups 1.178.0 / 1.179.0. |

(2)(3) `receiptLines` and the cost card: `V.itemLink(name, item_id)` (item 0 stays text); `Sales::get()` lines carry `category` via `line_categories()` (one IN query, the product's category now), shown as `vgc-sm-itemkind` under the name. (4) `by_category[].items` from `category_items()` (GROUP BY category_id, item_id; same rules as by_category; qty desc); `categoryCard(rows, open)`: a name with products is a button toggling `#catitems-{id}` in place, `salesOpenCats` keeps it open across a re-sort. | **1.172.0** | **Six small improvements.** (1) `#/locations` tabs when there are events — `locGroup()`: places (not `is_event`), current (event_state upcoming/running/to_settle; soonest first), past (settled; latest first); tab in the hash (`?tab=current|past`, `viewLocations(query)`), the event page's back link names its tab. (2) Sales opened with `?location=` shows `sales-back` to `#/location/{id}`. (3) Till `backUrl()` = appUrl + `?vgc_sm_co={company}` + `#/location/{locId}` (desk link and phone arrow). (4) `V.itemKind(row)` in core — *category · Product/Material/Service* (category first; reads `category_name` or `category`, `for_sale` as bool/int/string, `is_service`; '' when nothing known) — under names in the catalogue list (desk + phone; Made/Bought kept as a badge, the Category column removed), 12 item pickers (codemod: `esc(x.name) + (V.itemKind(x) || '<br>') + sku span`), the recipe inline search, a location's inventory/extras/services, custom products, the dashboard low-stock list. `shape_stock_row` now sends `for_sale` (every stock-row SELECT names `i.for_sale`); `extras()` sends `category`. Document lines (orders, purchases, notes, sale lines) and report tables carry no category and are unchanged. (5) `Sales::recent()` returns `gaps` via `price_gaps()`: per item over the filtered, non-voided sales (GROUP BY item, COUNT DISTINCT sales, MIN(is_extra)) — `no_price` when sold as a product (not only as an extra) with `price_net` empty/0, `no_cost` only for a manager and not for a service (`unit_cost_of` ≤ 0); rendered as `sales-gaps`. New `Sales::bind()` prepares only when there are params: the unfiltered list's agg/series (and the new query) called `prepare()` without placeholders — a WP notice per visit, found by the staging probe. (6) Catalogue list columns **B2C price** / **B2B price** (net; `priceCell`: a warning icon where `for_sale` and none set; B2B not required of a service; a material gets a dash; B2B column only for `canWrite()`, since viewers are not sent it); sort keys `b2c`/`b2b`. | **1.171.0** | **Quick extras at checkout (the bag, the small gift).** DB 0.60.0 → **0.61.0**: new per-company table `location_extras` (location_id, item_id, `price` DECIMAL NULL = free, created_at; PK location+item) — in `create_tables`, the runtime location-table repair, `Reset::kept_tables` (catalogue data) and **uninstall.php**; `sale_lines.is_extra` TINYINT. `VGC_SM_Locations::extras()/extra_prices()/add_extra()/remove_extra()` (any active non-service item; price read with a comma, negatives refused, '' = free). Routes `GET|POST /locations/{id}/extras`, `POST|DELETE /locations/{id}/extras/{item}` (read $auth, change $mgr). The till's `/locations/{id}/inventory` carries `extras`, and with `till=1` drops rows for extras that are not `for_sale` (a packing bag); a for-sale product given as an extra stays on the wall at its own price. **Checkout**: a line with `extra` is believed only for an item in `extra_prices()` here — then it is auto-DECLARED (never blocked; the shortfall goes to `VGC_SM_Backlog` like a ticked line), its list price (`orig_price`, and the default `unit_price`) is the extra's price here, and `is_extra = 1`; anything else is an ordinary line checked as one. `Sales::get` lines carry `is_extra`. Receipt template leaves off lines with `is_extra` and unit_price 0; the sale page tags them *Extra* / *Extra · free*. Till: `state.extras`, `cartIndex(item, extra)` (sold vs given are two lines), `addExtra()` → `refreshSale()`, extras skip `lineShort`/`heldPart`/`cartQtyMap`, `bindFoot()` binds discounts + extras + Charge, `T.extrasRow` above the totals (`pos-extras`), extra lines have no price box or discount. Location page: `location-extras` card via module-level `bindExtras()`. **Also fixed:** the var-hoisting reset of `offered` in `viewLocation` (services picker offered what was already on the menu).
